    MightNotTerminate,                // decreases variant not well-founded / non-decreasing
}

/// Source-level witness: variable bindings (mapped back from the Model) + the failing point.
pub struct Counterexample {
    pub bindings: Vec<(Atom, Value)>, // surface var name -> concrete value
    pub at: Span,                     // the program point that fails (== VError.span)
}

Driver contract (## 20–23): per method, desugar_method -> passify -> wp(_, True) -> encode -> SmtSolver::check(&[NOT VC]). Unsat => Verified; Sat(model) => VError with kind/span from the failing Assert's Reason and a Counterexample reconstructed by inverting DSA/skolem names; Unknown => VError (e.g. surfaced as a timeout, never Verified). Methods verified in parallel; each worker owns one SmtSolver session.

11.8 Type ownership & lifetime summary

Type Crate Owner / built by Mutability Sharing / concurrency Serialization
Program, Decl, Method, Spec, Stmt, Expr, Type, Param, Block, LValue spv-syntax Parser immutable post-parse &Program lent to all later stages; read-only => freely &-shared across threads print-ast
ParseError, ParseErrorKind spv-syntax Parser (Err path) value returned, not shared diagnostic text
Typed, TypeError spv-ast TypeChecker immutable tables per-program, read-only diagnostic text
IvlCmd, Reason spv-ivl desugar_method immutable post-desugar one tree per method; owned by that method's worker print-ivl
VcFormula, Pred spv-vc wp / passify immutable per-method; owned by worker print-vc
SmtSort, SmtTerm spv-smt encoder immutable per-method SMT-LIB 2 (print-smt)
SmtSolver session spv-smt impl ctor mutable (&mut self) single-threaded; one per worker; never shared SMT-LIB 2 wire
SmtResult, Model spv-smt check value returned to worker parsed from get-model
VerifyResult, VError, VErrorKind, Counterexample spv-verify driver value per-method, joined into Vec human-readable report

Invariant chain (each stage preserves the previous): well-typed AST -> well-formed IvlCmd (every Assert has a Reason) -> passify'd IVL (no Assign) -> VcFormula (closed; bound Vars under quantifiers) -> sorted SmtTerm (sort-correct) -> verdict. Breaking any link (a dropped Assert, a mis-framed Havoc, a sort error) is a soundness defect — the gravest failure (## 25 gate).

12. Phase 0 — Skeleton, workspace, and the "assert true verifies, assert false fails" smoke test

End-to-end vertical slice through all seven crates with type shells + a stub solver, so every later phase fills a hole, never invents the frame. The pipeline source.spv -> Program -> IvlCmd -> VcFormula -> SmtTerm -> SmtSolver.check -> VerifyResult must run today on a two-method input.

12.1 Objectives

  • Stand up spv Cargo workspace, 7 spv-* members (spv-syntax, spv-ast, spv-ivl, spv-vc, spv-smt, spv-verify, spv-cli), edition 2021, MSRV 1.78 pinned in rust-toolchain.toml.
  • Define type shells (variants present, bodies todo!()/trivial): Program/Decl/Method/Spec/Stmt/Expr/Type, IvlCmd, VcFormula, SmtTerm/SmtSort, SmtSolver trait + SmtResult/Model, VerifyResult/VError/VErrorKind/Counterexample, Reason. Error enums ParseErrorKind/TypeError.
  • One thin true path wired through all crates: hand-built assert true/assert false method -> desugar -> wp -> trivial VcFormula -> SmtTerm -> stub solver returns Unsat/Sat.
  • Ship smoke test: assert true ⇒ Verified; assert false ⇒ Failed([VError{kind: AssertionMightNotHold,..}]). Soundness invariant born here: a Sat model can NEVER map to Verified.
  • Wire CI (fmt/clippy -D warnings/build/test on MSRV+stable), Makefile, Dockerfile; spv-smt SmtSolver stub behind feature = "stub-solver"; scaffold Boogie/Dafny differential oracle (skipped if absent).

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

  • Workspace root (ADR-01, ADR-02): Cargo.toml [workspace] members=["crates/spv-syntax", ... "crates/spv-cli"], resolver="2", [workspace.package] shared version/edition/rust-version, [workspace.lints] (unsafe_code="forbid", clippy::pedantic="warn"). rust-toolchain.toml channel 1.78.
  • no_std layering: spv-syntax/spv-ast/spv-ivl/spv-vc = #![no_std] extern crate alloc; (deterministic, embeddable VCGen). spv-smt no_std core + std feature for I/O. spv-verify/spv-cli = std. Atom = interned symbol; Phase 0 alias pub type Atom = alloc::string::String; (real interner = Phase 1). Span = (u32,u32) byte range.
  • spv-syntax modules token.rs (Token), lexer.rs (Lexer shell), parser.rs (Parser shell, parse_program(&str)->Result<Program,ParseError> = todo!()), ast.rs (Program/Decl/Method/Spec/Stmt/Expr/Type/Param/LValue/Block/Value/UnOp/BinOp/Quantifier/Trigger), error.rs (ParseError{kind:ParseErrorKind,span:Span}).
  • spv-ast ty.rs (Type), resolve.rs (resolve(Program)->... shell), typeck.rs (TypeChecker, check(&Program)->Result<(),Vec<TypeError>> = Ok(()) shell), error.rs (TypeError). Re-export Type here per spine (single owner).
  • spv-ivl cmd.rs (IvlCmd, Reason{kind:VErrorKind,span:Span}, Var), desugar.rs (desugar_method(&Method)->IvlCmd — Phase 0: Stmt::Assert(e)->IvlCmd::Assert(e.clone(),Reason{AssertionMightNotHold,span}), else IvlCmd::Seq(vec![]); assume pre/assert post wrapping = Phase 5 stub).
  • spv-vc formula.rs (VcFormula, Pred), wp.rs (wp(&IvlCmd,VcFormula)->VcFormula — implement only Assume/Assert/Seq of the 5 rules; Havoc/Assign/Choice = todo!("Phase 7")), passify.rs (passify(&IvlCmd)->IvlCmd = identity shell). VErrorKind lives in spv-verify; spv-ivl/spv-vc import it (no dup).
  • spv-smt sort.rs (SmtSort), term.rs (SmtTerm), encode.rs (encode(&VcFormula)->SmtTerm: True->SmtTerm::True, False->SmtTerm::False, else todo!()), emit.rs (to_smtlib(&[SmtTerm])->String, gated std), solver.rs (SmtSolver trait, SmtResult, Model), stub.rs (StubSolver under feature="stub-solver": returns Unsat iff every assertion is syntactically SmtTerm::True/closed-trivially-valid, else Sat(Model::empty()); never uses heuristics that could call a refutable goal valid).
  • spv-verify verify.rs verify_method(&Method,&mut dyn SmtSolver)->VerifyResult: c=desugar_method(m); c=passify(&c); vc=wp(&c,VcFormula::True); t=encode_negation(&vc); match solver.check(&[t]) { Unsat=>Verified, Sat(m)=>Failed(reconstruct(m,&vc)), Unknown=>Failed(..MightNotTerminate stub) }. error.rs (VError/VErrorKind/Counterexample), recon.rs (reconstruct shell -> single VError from Reason). encode_negation = SmtTerm::Not(encode(vc)).
  • spv-cli bin/spv.rs clap subcommands verify|print-ast|print-ivl|print-vc|print-smt; Phase 0 verify ignores file path, runs hard-coded fixtures (real parse = Phase 1), prints VerifyResult + exit code (0 verified / 1 failed / 2 usage). Other subcommands print "unimplemented (phase N)".
  • CI/build: .github/workflows/ci.yml jobs fmt(cargo fmt --check), clippy(-D warnings), build+test matrix {1.78, stable}. Makefile targets fmt check clippy build test smoke doc clean ci. Dockerfile rust:1.78-slim multi-stage -> distroless spv. xtask/scripts/diff-oracle.sh = Boogie/Dafny differential scaffold (locates boogie/dafny; missing ⇒ emit SKIP, exit 0).

12.3 Deliverable

cargo build --workspace + cargo test --workspace --features spv-smt/stub-solver green on 1.78 and stable; make ci passes. spv verify exits 0 on the assert true fixture, 1 (printing an AssertionMightNotHold VError) on assert false. All 7 crates compile with shells; make smoke runs the two-method slice end-to-end.

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

# Criterion Measure
A1 Workspace builds cargo build --workspace exit 0, 7 crates, on 1.78 + stable
A2 Smoke passes assert true⇒Verified; assert false⇒Failed w/ kind==AssertionMightNotHold
A3 Soundness guard property test: any input where stub returns Sat ⇒ result ≠ Verified (0 false-verifieds)
A4 Lint-clean cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings exit 0; cargo fmt --check clean
A5 no_std holds spv-vc builds with --no-default-features (no accidental std)
A6 CLI contract spv verify exit codes {0,1,2}; print-* exit 0 w/ "unimplemented" stub
A7 Cold build full clean workspace build ≤ 60 s on 4-core CI runner

12.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Input Expected
fixtures/smoke/assert_true.spv method M() { assert true; } Verified, exit 0
fixtures/smoke/assert_false.spv method M() { assert false; } Failed([{AssertionMightNotHold, span≈assert}]), exit 1
fixtures/smoke/empty.spv method M() { } Verified (empty body ⇒ wp=true)
fixtures/smoke/two_asserts.spv assert true; assert false; Failed, single VError at 2nd assert
fixtures/malformed/garbage.spv non-program bytes clean error (ParseError), exit 2, no panic
fixtures/malformed/empty_file.spv 0 bytes Program{decls:[]} ⇒ Verified, exit 0
  • Golden expectations in fixtures/*/expected.txt; make smoke diffs CLI stdout. (Phase 0 wires fixtures via hard-coded ASTs; Phase 1 replaces with real parse — fixture files + expected outputs stay stable.)

12.6 Pitfalls

  • todo!()/unimplemented!() on the hot path ⇒ runtime panic masked as a verifier crash. Root cause: shell reached by a real input. Debug: RUST_BACKTRACE=1; gate the slice so only Assert/Seq/True/False are exercised in Phase 0; CI catches via A1/A2.
  • Stub solver too clever ⇒ false Verified (gravest failure, A3). Root cause: heuristic guessing validity. Rule: StubSolver returns Unsat only for syntactically-trivially-valid goals (negation reduces to False); everything else ⇒ Sat. Conservative bias preserves soundness even while incomplete.
  • VErrorKind defined twice (in spv-ivl for Reason and spv-verify) ⇒ type mismatch at the Reason->VError boundary. Root cause: layering. Fix: single owner = spv-verify; spv-ivl/spv-vc depend on it (or hoist to a tiny spv-verify re-export). Compiler error E0308 at recon.rs.
  • Accidental std in no_std crate ⇒ A5 fails on embedded targets later. Root cause: std:: path or a std-default dep. Debug: cargo build -p spv-vc --no-default-features; check default-features=false on every dep; #![no_std] at crate root.
  • encode_negation negates wrong polarity ⇒ verify/refute inverted (assert true reports Failed). Root cause: VC-valid ⟺ ¬VC-unsat confusion. Invariant tattooed here: solver checks NOT VC; Unsat⇒Verified. A2 the two-direction test catches sign flips.
  • MSRV drift ⇒ green locally, red on 1.78. Root cause: edition-2024/newer-stdlib feature. Debug: rust-toolchain.toml + CI 1.78 leg; avoid let-else-recent APIs.

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • Phase 0 has no perf goals beyond build hygiene: cold workspace build ≤ 60 s (A7), incremental ≤ 5 s, make smoke < 1 s wall.
  • Keep IR enums small: Box recursive arms (Expr, IvlCmd::Choice, VcFormula) so size_of stays ≤ 32 B — large enums silently bloat Vec<Stmt>/wp later.
  • Atom=String is a temporary allocation cost; flag for Phase 1 interning (target: Atom=u32 symbol, Vec<Stmt> cache-dense). Don't optimize now; just don't bake String assumptions into APIs (pass &Atom).

12.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty Program{decls:[]} and empty method body ⇒ wp=VcFormula::True ⇒ Verified (vacuous, correct).
  • Method{body: None} (abstract/extern) ⇒ Phase 0 treats as Verified (contract-only; real handling Phase 5).
  • Nested Seq(vec![]) / single-element Seq ⇒ wp must be associative & identity-correct (wp(Seq[],Q)=Q).
  • assert false; assert true; ⇒ failure attributed to the first failing assert via its Reason.span, not the last.
  • UTF-8 / BOM / CRLF in .spv files ⇒ reader must not panic (Phase 0 only on malformed fixtures; lexer owns this Phase 1).

12.9 Failure Analysis

Mode Detection Mitigation
False Verified (Sat→Verified leak) A3 property test, smoke A2 conservative stub; assert result≠Verified on any Sat in verify_method debug-assert
Panic via todo!() reached CI A1/A2, fuzz-lite on malformed scope Phase-0 inputs to wired variants; #[should_panic] quarantine for known holes
Polarity inversion two-direction smoke (true & false) single encode_negation chokepoint, unit-tested both ways
Layering cycle (spv-ivl↔spv-verify) cargo build E0391 VErrorKind in a leaf/lowest crate; strict dependency DAG (spine order)
Non-determinism (HashMap iteration in output) golden-diff flakiness BTreeMap/sorted output in print-* + Model rendering

12.10 Operational Notes

  • make smoke is the local pre-commit gate; make ci mirrors GitHub exactly (fmt → clippy → build → test). Solver feature: --features spv-smt/stub-solver default in CI; real backend (#278/Z3) lands Phase 10 behind same SmtSolver trait.
  • Differential oracle (scripts/diff-oracle.sh): probes which boogie dafny; absent ⇒ logs SKIP boogie/dafny not found, exit 0 (never fails CI). Activated Phase 13.
  • Determinism: no wall-clock/RNG/HashMap-iteration in any pipeline output; counterexample/model rendering sorted, so goldens are byte-stable across runs/platforms.
  • Versioning: workspace single-version; tag v0.0.1-skeleton once A1–A7 pass — the frozen frame all later phases build into.

13. Phase 1 — Source front end (lexer, parser, AST)

13.1 Objectives

  • Build spv-syntax: Lexer (UTF-8 source → Vec<Token> w/ spans) + recursive-descent Parser → Program AST. no_std + alloc.
  • Surface AST per spine: Program/Decl/Method/Function/Spec/Stmt/Expr/Type — methods w/ requires/ensures/modifies/decreases; stmts assert/assume/var/assign/if/while(+invariants+decreases)/call/return; exprs incl old(), Quant w/ Trigger, function Call, Field.
  • Emit precise ParseError{kind: ParseErrorKind, span: Span} (UnexpectedToken/BadSpec/BadType); recover to next statement/decl for multi-error reporting.
  • Scope guard: syntax + tree shape ONLY. No name resolution, no Type checking, no Bool-ness of specs (Phase 2). Atom = interned identifier; declared-vs-used unchecked here.

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Crate spv-syntax (ADR-03: hand-written lexer/parser, not a generator), files under crates/spv-syntax/src/:

File Contents
lexer.rs Token, TokenKind, Lexer::new(&str)->Lexer, Lexer::next()->Token, span tracking (u32 byte offsets)
token.rs enum TokenKind (kw/punct/ident/lit/eof), keyword table, Atom interner
ast.rs Program/Decl/Method/Function/Param/Spec/Block/Stmt/Expr/LValue/Type/Value/UnOp/BinOp/Quantifier/Trigger
parser.rs Parser{toks, pos}, parse_program(&str)->Result<Program,Vec<ParseError>>, descent fns
error.rs ParseError{kind, span, found: TokenKind, expected: &'static str}, ParseErrorKind
lib.rs re-exports; pub fn parse(src:&str)->Result<Program,Vec<ParseError>>
  • Lexer: longest-match; // line + /* */ block comments skipped; keyword vs ident disambiguated via table; numeric Lit → Value::Int(i128); true/false→Value::Bool.
  • Parser: Pratt/precedence-climbing for Expr (binding-power table); statement-keyword dispatch for Stmt; Block = { stmts }.
  • old(e) → Expr::Old(Box<e>); forall/exists → Expr::Quant(q, binders, triggers, body) with { e1, e2 } trigger lists pre-body; f(args) ident-then-( → Expr::Call.
  • Spec clauses parsed as repeated requires|ensures|modifies|decreases <expr>; between method header and body; aggregated into Spec (Vec per clause).
  • Error recovery: on UnexpectedToken, synchronize() skips to next ;/}/decl-keyword, push error, continue → collect multiple ParseError. Token kinds (LOCKED keyword set) + EBNF (drives parser.rs, names match AST exactly):
keywords  := method | function | returns | requires | ensures | modifies | decreases
           | var | assert | assume | if | else | while | invariant | call | return
           | forall | exists | old | axiom | true | false | Int | Bool | Ref | Map | Seq
punct     := ( ) { } [ ] , ; : :: := == != <= >= < > + - * / % && || ! ==> <==> . @
Program  ::= Decl*
Decl     ::= Method | Function | "axiom" Expr ";"
Method   ::= "method" Atom "(" Params? ")" ("returns" "(" Params? ")")? Spec Block?
Function ::= "function" Atom "(" Params? ")" ":" Type Spec ("{" Expr "}")?
Params   ::= Param ("," Param)*          Param ::= Atom ":" Type
Spec     ::= ( ("requires"|"ensures"|"modifies"|"decreases") Expr ";" )*
Block    ::= "{" Stmt* "}"
Stmt     ::= "var" Atom ":" Type (":=" Expr)? ";"
           | LValue ":=" Expr ";"
           | "if" Expr Block ("else" Block)?
           | "while" Expr ("invariant" Expr ";" | "decreases" Expr ";")* Block
           | "call" (LValues ":=")? Atom "(" Args? ")" ";"
           | "assert" Expr ";" | "assume" Expr ";" | "return" ";"
LValue   ::= Atom | Expr "." Atom
Expr     ::= Quant | Imp
Quant    ::= ("forall"|"exists") Param ("," Param)* Trigger* "::" Expr
Trigger  ::= "{" Expr ("," Expr)* "}"
Imp      ::= Or (("==>"|"<==>") Or)*       Or ::= And ("||" And)*
And      ::= Cmp ("&&" Cmp)*               Cmp::= Add (("=="|"!="|"<"|"<="|">"|">=") Add)*
Add      ::= Mul (("+"|"-") Mul)*          Mul::= Unary (("*"|"/"|"%") Unary)*
Unary    ::= ("!"|"-") Unary | Postfix
Postfix  ::= Primary ("." Atom)*
Primary  ::= Literal | "old" "(" Expr ")" | Atom "(" Args? ")" | Atom | "(" Expr ")"

Precedence (low→high): <==> < ==> < || < && < cmp < + - < * / % < unary < postfix .. ==> right-assoc.

13.3 Deliverable

spv-syntax crate + spv print-ast <file.spv> (via spv-cli shim) parses arbitrary annotated programs → Program AST, pretty-prints it, and reports multiple ParseErrors with span + expected for malformed input. cargo test -p spv-syntax green.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

# Criterion Measure
A1 All EBNF forms parse every grammar rule covered by ≥1 fixture
A2 Span accuracy each AST node's span ⊆ source; round-trips to correct byte range
A3 Multi-error ≥3 independent syntax errors in one file all reported (recovery works)
A4 print-ast stable byte-identical re-print across runs; idempotent re-parse of pretty output
A5 Throughput ≥ 5 MB/s lex+parse; 10 k-line program < 200 ms
A6 Zero panics fuzzer (1 M inputs) yields only Err(Vec<ParseError>), never panic/UB

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expects
tests/fixtures/ok/abs.spv method abs w/ requires/ensures, if/else, return → full AST
tests/fixtures/ok/loop_sum.spv while w/ 2 invariant + decreases → Stmt::While{invariants,decreases,..}
tests/fixtures/ok/quant.spv forall i:Int { f(i) } :: 0<=i ==> p(i) → Expr::Quant w/ 1 Trigger
tests/fixtures/ok/old_call.spv ensures x == old(x)+1; + call r := m(a); → Old, Stmt::Call
tests/fixtures/ok/types.spv Map<Int,Ref>, Seq<Int>, Named → Type variants
tests/fixtures/err/missing_semi.spv BadSpec/UnexpectedToken at ; slot; recovery continues
tests/fixtures/err/bad_type.spv var x: Mapp; → BadType span on Mapp
tests/fixtures/err/unclosed.spv unterminated {//* → UnexpectedToken at EOF, no hang
tests/fixtures/err/old_no_arg.spv old) → UnexpectedToken expecting ( (each ok/*.spv paired w/ .ast golden snapshot; each err/*.spv paired w/ expected (kind, span, expected) list)

13.6 Pitfalls

  • Quantifier :: vs type : — forall i:Int :: body shares : token; root cause: greedy binder parse swallows ::. Fix: parse binders until token is {(trigger) or ::; debug by dumping token stream around binder.
  • ==> vs == vs = lex split — naive char-by-char yields == then >; root cause: not longest-match. Fix: maximal-munch in lexer; assert Token for a==>b is single Imp.
  • old / quantifier kw as ident — var old: Int; mis-lexed as keyword. Decision: old/forall/exists are reserved words → reject as ident with clear BadSpec; document in glossary.
  • Trigger braces vs block braces — { f(i) } (trigger) vs { stmts } (block) both {; root cause: context-free brace. Fix: triggers only parsed inside Quant after binders, before ::.
  • Unary minus vs binary minus — -1 literal vs a - 1; root cause: ambiguous -. Fix: Unary rule consumes leading -; precedence table separates; test a - -1.
  • Right-assoc ==> — a==>b==>c must parse a==>(b==>c); root cause: left-fold default. Fix: explicit right recursion; golden AST check.
  • Infinite recovery loop — synchronize() not advancing past the offending token → spin. Fix: always consume ≥1 token before re-attempt; fuzz with adversarial ;;;;.

13.7 Performance Concerns

  • Single-pass lexer, no backtracking; Token stored in Vec (not re-lexed). Target ≥ 5 MB/s.
  • Atom interning via FxHashMap<&str,u32> → O(1) ident compare downstream; avoids String clones in AST.
  • AST built directly into Box/Vec (no intermediate parse-tree); expected ≤ 3× source bytes resident.
  • Pratt parser bounded recursion depth = expression nesting; guard with explicit depth counter (cap 512) to bound stack (see 13.8).

13.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty Program (0 decls) → Program{decls:vec![]} Ok.
  • Method w/o body (Method.body == None) — contract-only declaration; legal here.
  • Empty Spec (no clauses) → all four Vecs empty.
  • Deeply nested expr (((((...)))) ×1000) → depth-cap ParseError, not stack overflow.
  • Trailing comma in params/args/triggers → UnexpectedToken (not silently accepted).
  • Numeric literal overflow (> i128::MAX) → BadType/lex error w/ span, not wrap.
  • Unicode in idents (non-ASCII) → decision: ASCII-only idents v1; non-ASCII → lex error. decreases on non-loop / multiple decreases on a method → accepted syntactically; semantics deferred to Phase 2/6.

13.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Consequence Mitigation
Wrong precedence/assoc golden AST diff mis-parsed VC later → false verify (soundness!) EBNF-locked table + A1 coverage
Dropped spec clause fixture old_call missing ensures → unsound aggregate-then-assert clause counts
Span off-by-one A2 round-trip bad counterexample location (Phase 11) byte-exact span tests
Panic on malformed A6 fuzz crash on untrusted input Result everywhere, no unwrap in parser

13.10 Operational Notes

  • spv print-ast file.spv = human-debug surface; --json optional for tooling.
  • Diagnostics: file:line:col: error[BadSpec]: expected ';', found 'ensures' w/ caret span; multiple errors batched.
  • Determinism: parse output a pure function of bytes; no env/locale/time dependence (reproducible goldens in CI).
  • no_std+alloc: lexer/parser usable from any host; std only in spv-cli. MSRV 1.78.
  • Snapshot tests via insta-style goldens; regen gated behind explicit UPDATE_SNAPSHOTS=1 to prevent accidental rebaseline.

14. Phase 2 — Name resolution, type checker, spec well-formedness

Implements spv-ast: turn the surface Program from Phase 1 into a resolved, typed AST or a precise TypeError. Three passes — (1) name resolution (identifier → declaration), (2) TypeChecker (infer/check Type ∈ {Int,Bool,Ref,Map,Seq,Named}), (3) spec well-formedness (specs Bool; old() only in ensures; modifies well-formed lvalues; call/function arity). Gate: no IVL/VC work touches an unchecked AST — soundness depends on it.

14.1 Objectives

  • Resolve every Var(Atom)/Call(Atom,..)/Field(_,Atom) to a declaration; emit TypeError::Undeclared on miss; reject duplicate decls/params/locals.
  • Infer & check Type for every Expr; verify assignment/contract/operator typing; emit Mismatch{expected,got}/ArityMismatch.
  • Spec well-formedness: requires/ensures/invariants are Bool (NonBoolSpec); Old only in ensures (OldOutsideEnsures); modifies entries are assignable lvalues over Ref/Map state (BadModifies).
  • Produce a typed AST (every Expr annotated with its Type, every name carrying a DefId) consumed unchanged by spv-ivl/spv-vc.
  • Determinism: same input → byte-identical typed AST + first error; errors ordered by source Span.

14.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Crate spv-ast (ADR-04: one crate owns resolution + typing + spec WF; no_std + alloc, depends on spv-syntax). Modules under crates/spv-ast/src/:

File Contents
lib.rs re-exports Type, TypeError, resolve_and_check(&Program) -> Result<TypedProgram, Vec<TypeError>>
types.rs pub enum Type { Int, Bool, Ref, Map(Box<Type>,Box<Type>), Seq(Box<Type>), Named(Atom) }; Type::eq, Type::compatible, Display
error.rs pub enum TypeError { Undeclared(Atom), Mismatch{expected:Type,got:Type}, NonBoolSpec, OldOutsideEnsures, BadModifies, ArityMismatch } + Span per variant
defid.rs pub struct DefId(u32); pub enum Def { Method(DefId), Function(DefId), Param{ty:Type}, Local{ty:Type}, TypeDecl }
scope.rs Scope = Vec<HashMap<Atom,DefId>> (lexical stack); enter()/exit()/declare(Atom)->Result<DefId,TypeError>/lookup(Atom)->Option<DefId>
globals.rs pass-1 collect_globals(&Program) -> GlobalEnv{ methods:Map<Atom,MethodSig>, functions:Map<Atom,FnSig>, types:Set<Atom> }; signature MethodSig{params:Vec<Type>,returns:Vec<Type>}, FnSig{params:Vec<Type>,ret:Type}
resolve.rs pass-2 walk: bind params→locals→nested blocks; rewrite each Atom ref to DefId; Field resolution deferred to typeck (needs receiver Type)
typeck.rs TypeChecker{ genv, scope, in_ensures:bool, ret_tys:&[Type] }; infer_expr(&Expr)->Result<Type,TypeError>; check_expr(&Expr, expected); check_stmt, check_method, check_function, check_spec
wf.rs spec well-formedness: check_bool_clauses, check_old_placement, check_modifies
typed.rs TypedProgram, TypedExpr{ node:ExprKind, ty:Type, def:Option<DefId> }, TypedMethod, … (1:1 mirror of surface AST + ty/def)
Pipeline resolve_and_check: collect_globals → for each Decl: resolve body → TypeChecker::check_method/check_function/check Axiom is Bool & closed → on each error push to Vec, resync at next stmt/decl to report multiple errors per run.
Operator typing (infer_expr for Bin/Un):
Form Operand types
------ ---------------
+ - * / % Int,Int
< <= > >= Int,Int
== != T,T (same Type::eq)
`&&
! Bool
- (neg) Int
Map[k] (Bin index) Map(K,V), K
|s| (seq len) Seq(_)
Field(e,f) Ref receiver, field f of decl
Quant(_,binders,trigs,b) b:Bool (binders pushed to scope)
Old(e) type of e, two-state
Call(f,args) FnSig.params ↔ args
stmt Call(outs,m,args) MethodSig params↔args, returns↔outs
check_stmt: VarDecl(a,t,init?) → declare a:t, if init check_expr(init,t); Assign(lv,e) → check_expr(e, type_of_lvalue(lv)); If(c,_,_)/While{guard,..} → guard Bool; Assert/Assume(e) → e Bool; Call arity+types; Return → arity vs ret_tys.

14.3 Deliverable

resolve_and_check(&Program) returns TypedProgram (every Expr carries Type, every name a DefId) for well-formed input, else Vec<TypeError> with spans. spv-cli print-ast prints the typed AST showing inferred types and resolved DefIds. No code below spv-ast ever sees an unchecked AST.

14.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Corpus: ≥ 40 well-typed programs → Ok, typed AST round-trips (print-ast stable, idempotent re-check).
  • ≥ 40 ill-typed programs → exact TypeError variant + span (golden-matched), one per seeded fault.
  • Every node in a typed AST has a non-None Type; every Var/Call/Field has a resolved DefId (debug-assert sweep).
  • All six TypeError variants exercised ≥ 3×; multi-error resync yields ≥ 2 errors on a doubly-broken file.
  • Throughput ≥ 50k AST nodes / s; resolve+typeck < 5 % of total verify wall-time on the Phase 13 suite.

14.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expect
ok_arith_bool.spv Ok; x+1 : Int, x<y : Bool
ok_old_in_ensures.spv Ok; ensures r == old(x)+1
ok_map_seq.spv Ok; m[k]:V, |s|:Int, Map(Int,Bool) decl
ok_quant_trigger.spv Ok; forall i:Int {f(i)} :: f(i) >= 0 Bool
err_undeclared.spv Undeclared(y) @ use site
err_mismatch_assign.spv Mismatch{expected:Int,got:Bool}
err_nonbool_requires.spv NonBoolSpec @ requires x+1
err_old_in_requires.spv OldOutsideEnsures @ requires old(x)
err_bad_modifies.spv BadModifies @ modifies x+1 (non-lvalue)
err_call_arity.spv ArityMismatch callee 2 params, 3 args
err_return_arity.spv ArityMismatch 2 returns, Return with 1
err_dup_param.spv Undeclared-class dup → declare error @ 2nd p
Malformed err_quant_nonbool_body.spv → NonBoolSpec; err_index_nonmap.spv (Int[k]) → Mismatch; err_field_on_int.spv → Mismatch; err_old_nested_in_old.spv → Ok (idempotent) but flagged in print-ast
Each fixture pairs .spv + .expected (typed-AST dump or TypeError debug string); CI diffs them.

14.6 Pitfalls

  • old() scoping inside quantifiers in ensures: bound vars of a Quant are current-state; old(i) where i is a quantifier binder is meaningless. Root cause: in_ensures flag set but binder-vs-program-var not distinguished. Debug: tag each Atom with Def::Param/Local vs binder; forbid Old over binders.
  • Map/Seq structural equality: Type::eq must recurse (Map(Int,Bool) != Map(Int,Int)); a shallow tag-only compare passes wrong index types → unsound downstream SMT sort. Debug: property test eq against a canonical Display.
  • Resolution before global collection: forward calls (m calls n declared later) fail if pass-1 collect_globals is skipped. Root cause: single-pass walk. Debug: assert genv populated before any resolve.
  • modifies of a local: modifies may only name heap/Ref state, never a method local/param. Misclassifying a local as a valid frame target silently widens the frame → havoc of nothing or over-havoc later. Debug: check_modifies consults Def kind, rejects Local.
  • ==/!= over non-comparable types: equality must require identical Type; allowing Int == Bool to typecheck yields a malformed SMT atom. Debug: Type::eq guard in equality arm with span-precise Mismatch.
  • Shadowing across blocks: re-declaring a name in a nested Block must shadow, not duplicate-error; conflating "duplicate in same scope" with "shadow in child scope" rejects valid programs. Debug: Scope per-frame dup check only against the top frame.

14.7 Performance Concerns

  • Two linear passes (resolve, typeck) → O(N) in AST nodes; Scope::lookup O(depth) — keep depth small via flat block frames.
  • Atom interning (from spv-syntax) makes scope keys u32 compares; no string hashing in hot path.
  • Intern Type nodes / share Box<Type> for Int/Bool singletons to cut alloc; Type::eq short-circuits on tag.
  • Target: 100k-node program resolved+typed < 50 ms, < 16 bytes overhead/node (ty tag + Option<DefId>).
  • Multi-error mode bounded: cap reported errors at 100 to avoid quadratic resync on adversarial input.

14.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty Program (no decls) → Ok empty TypedProgram.
  • Method with body: None (abstract/external) → check spec well-formedness only, skip body resolution.
  • Nested Old(Old(e)) → idempotent (= Old(e)); allow, normalize in typed.rs.
  • Quantifier with empty trigger list → Ok (triggers optional; inference deferred to spv-smt Phase 9).
  • Named(T) unknown type → Undeclared(T); Named referencing a future datatype (Phase 12) → reserved, currently Undeclared.
  • Self-referential function f calling f (recursion) → Ok at typeck (well-foundedness/fuel is Phase 12, not here).
  • Zero-returns method Call with empty out-list → Ok; mismatch only on count or type.
  • modifies empty → Ok (pure-by-frame method).

14.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Severity Mitigation
Type error missed → unchecked AST reaches spv-ivl critical (unsoundness root) exhaustive match, debug-assert all nodes typed before return; fuzz typeck
Old allowed outside ensures high (wrong two-state VC) in_ensures flag + wf::check_old_placement walk; fixture-gated
Structural Type::eq bug high (bad SMT sort) property tests, canonical Display round-trip
Wrong error span medium (bad UX) span propagated from surface node; golden span asserts
Panic on malformed AST (overflow DefId, deep nesting) medium DefId u32 with checked alloc; recursion-depth guard → TypeError not panic
Forward-reference miss medium (valid program rejected) mandatory pass-1 collect_globals

14.10 Operational Notes

  • spv-cli print-ast <file> dumps typed AST (--show-types, --show-defids); --max-errors N caps reporting.
  • TypeError Display is single-line file:line:col: <kind>: <detail>; debug form is golden-fixture string.
  • Determinism: HashMap replaced by BTreeMap/sorted iteration where order is observable (error list, print-ast).
  • Feature flag strict-modifies (default on) toggles BadModifies for modifies over locals — off only for diagnostics.
  • Logged metrics: nodes typed, errors emitted, resolve/typeck µs — surfaced under verify --stats.
  • Invariant assertion (debug builds): assert!(typed.all_nodes_typed() && typed.all_names_resolved()) before handing to spv-ivl.

15. Phase 3 — The logical expression language (FOL + old + quantifiers)

Scope: translate typed source Expr (from spv-ast) into the first-order term/atom language used by VC generation — arithmetic, booleans, equality, quantifiers + triggers, two-state old(), and pure-function applications. This is the semantic bridge between the surface AST and VcFormula/Pred; every downstream phase (wp, DSA, SMT) consumes the terms minted here. The translation is total on well-typed Expr (Phase 2 guarantees typing) and deterministic (no name capture, stable ordering). Output is pure logic, not IVL: no assume/assert/control flow — those arrive in Phases 4–6.

15.1 Objectives

  • Define a typed logical term layer LExpr (in spv-vc) and the predicate atom Pred embedded in VcFormula::Atom(Pred); provide translate_expr(&Expr, &TransCtx) -> LExpr and translate_formula(&Expr) -> VcFormula (boolean-typed Expr → formula; else → term).
  • Fix semantics for: Int arithmetic (+ - * / %, division/mod toward-zero, partiality of /0), Bool connectives (&& || ! ==> <==>), polymorphic equality == / != over Int/Bool/Ref/Map/Seq, ordering (< <= > >=).
  • Implement two-state old(e): bind every free program var in e to its pre-state version; legal only inside ensures (Phase 2 enforced) — translation assumes that invariant.
  • Implement quantifiers Quant(Forall|Exists, vars, triggers, body) → VcFormula::Forall/Exists with de-Bruijn-safe bound vars and trigger terms preserved verbatim for Phase 9 instantiation.
  • Translate pure-function calls Expr::Call(f, args) to uninterpreted applications LExpr::App(f, [..]); defining axioms are deferred to Phase 12 (this phase emits the symbol + sort only).
  • Establish the division-by-zero proof obligation hook: emit a side-condition DivisionByZero Pred so Phases 4/7 can assert it (this phase records, does not assert).

15.2 Detailed Implementation Steps (ADR-16 quantifier/trigger handling; B2/B3 first-order theory)

Crate spv-vc, new modules (per section 10 layout):

File Contents
spv-vc/src/lexpr.rs LExpr term enum + Pred atom + smart constructors (and_, imp_, eq_)
spv-vc/src/translate.rs translate_expr, translate_formula, TransCtx, BinOp/UnOp lowering tables
spv-vc/src/twostate.rs old() handling: StateTag::{Pre,Cur}, Var versioning by state
spv-vc/src/trigger.rs Trigger validation (no interpreted-only triggers), free-var coverage check

Logical term layer (Rust-ish signatures; Var carries source Atom + StateTag):

pub enum LExpr {                       // sorted FOL terms (pre-SMT, source-agnostic)
    Lit(Value),                        // Int / Bool literal
    Var(Var),                          // program var @ a StateTag (Pre|Cur)
    Bound(u32),                        // de-Bruijn index for quantified vars
    Un(UnOp, Box<LExpr>),
    Bin(BinOp, Box<LExpr>, Box<LExpr>),// arithmetic / comparison
    App(Atom, Vec<LExpr>),             // pure-function application = uninterpreted symbol
    Ite(Box<VcFormula>, Box<LExpr>, Box<LExpr>), // from interpreted Map.get / cond exprs
}
pub enum Pred {                        // atoms inside VcFormula::Atom
    BoolTerm(LExpr),                   // an LExpr of sort Bool used as a formula
    Cmp(CmpOp, LExpr, LExpr),          // ==, !=, <, <=, >, >=
    DivOk(LExpr),                      // side-cond: divisor != 0 (DivisionByZero hook)
}
pub struct TransCtx { pub state: StateTag, pub bound: Vec<(Atom, Type)>, pub funcs: FuncSigs }
pub enum StateTag { Pre, Cur }
  • translate_formula(e) requires typeof(e)==Bool: maps &&→And, ||→Or, !→Not, ==>→Imp, <==>→And([Imp,Imp]) (or a Bin(Iff,..) lowered to two Imp), comparisons → Atom(Cmp..), everything else → Atom(BoolTerm(translate_expr e)).
  • translate_expr(e) for non-Bool: literals/vars/Bin(arith)/App. Bin(Div|Mod,a,b) additionally registers a Pred::DivOk(b) obligation in a side-channel ctx.obligations keyed by span (consumed by Phase 4/7).
  • old(e): set ctx.state = Pre, recurse, restore. A free Var(x) under Pre becomes Var{x, Pre}; bound vars and literals are state-insensitive. Nested old(old(e)) collapses to Pre (idempotent).
  • Quantifiers: push (name,ty) onto ctx.bound; body free occurrences of those names lower to LExpr::Bound(depth); emit VcFormula::Forall(vars, triggers', body'). Triggers translated with the same ctx (so trigger terms reference bound vars correctly).
  • Capture avoidance: bound names are not α-renamed at translation (de-Bruijn Bound makes capture impossible); source Var always resolves to a free Var, never a Bound, because Phase 2 resolution already distinguished them.

15.3 Deliverable

translate_formula / translate_expr in spv-vc producing VcFormula/LExpr for any well-typed Expr, exercised through spv print-vc --expr-only (a debug path that translates the first method's ensures conjunction and pretty-prints the resulting VcFormula). Round-trips for arithmetic, equality, ==>, forall/exists with triggers, and old() produce stable, deterministic FOL output. No IVL, no SMT yet.

15.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • 100% totality: every Phase-2-typed Expr in the conformance corpus translates without panic; property test (10k random well-typed Expr) yields no unreachable!.
  • old() correctness: in a method ensures r == old(x) + 1, the formula contains exactly one Var{x, Pre} and one Var{r, Cur} (asserted by an AST-walk test).
  • Quantifier de-Bruijn: forall i:Int :: forall j:Int :: a[i]==a[j] translates with inner refs at Bound(0)/Bound(1); no name appears in LExpr::Var.
  • Trigger fidelity: trigger term set preserved 1:1 (count + structure) into VcFormula::Forall.
  • DivOk obligations: a body with n syntactic //% ops registers exactly n Pred::DivOk entries.
  • Determinism: translating the same Expr twice produces byte-identical pretty-print (golden file).

15.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input snippet Expected print-vc --expr-only
arith_eq.spv ensures r == (a + b) * 2 Atom(Cmp(Eq, Var{r,Cur}, Bin(Mul, Bin(Add, a, b), 2)))
imp_iff.spv ensures p ==> (q <==> r) Imp(p, And([Imp(q,r), Imp(r,q)]))
old_two_state.spv ensures x == old(x) + 1 one Var{x,Pre}, one Var{x,Cur}
forall_trig.spv forall i:Int {f(i)} :: f(i) >= 0 Forall([(i,Int)], [Trigger[App(f,[Bound 0])]], Atom(Cmp(Ge, App(f,[Bound 0]), 0)))
nested_old.spv ensures old(old(x)) == old(x) both sides Var{x,Pre} (collapse)
div_obl.spv ensures r == a / b 1 Pred::DivOk(b) registered
malformed old_in_req.spv requires old(x) > 0 rejected upstream in Phase 2 (OldOutsideEnsures) — translator never invoked; fixture asserts the pipeline error
malformed trig_lit.spv forall i:Int {i+1} :: i>=0 trigger.rs rejects interpreted-only trigger → diagnostic, no formula
malformed unbound_trig.spv forall i:Int {f(j)} :: ... trigger references non-bound j not in body coverage → rejected

15.6 Pitfalls

  • old() over a bound variable (forall i :: old(i)): meaningless — bound vars have no pre-state. Root cause: blindly applying StateTag::Pre to every Var. Fix: Pre tags only free program vars; Bound is state-insensitive. Debug: dump StateTag of every LExpr::Var; any Pre on a name in ctx.bound is the bug.
  • Trigger loops / matching loops: a trigger whose instantiation re-creates a matching term ({f(i)} :: f(i)==f(i+1)) makes Phase 9/10 diverge (Unknown/timeout). Root cause is not in translation but must be surfaced here: trigger.rs warns on triggers strictly contained in a produced term. Debug: log instantiation depth in Phase 10, trace back to the offending quantifier span.
  • Boolean term vs formula confusion: translating a Bool-typed Var as an LExpr then wrapping in BoolTerm vs. as a formula directly — both valid but must be consistent, else wp sees mixed shapes. Fix: single rule — Bool-typed Expr always goes through translate_formula; BoolTerm only for Bool subterms of a term context (e.g. arg to App).
  • Toward-zero vs floor division mismatch: % semantics must match the SMT encoding (Phase 9). Root cause: source spec says toward-zero (-7 / 2 == -3) but SMT div/mod is Euclidean. Fix: pin toward-zero here in BinOp::Div/Mod semantics; Phase 9 emits the matching div/mod definition. Mismatch ⇒ unsoundness (false verify). Debug: differential test -7/2, -7%2, 7/-2 vs reference.
  • Equality sort polymorphism: emitting Cmp(Eq,..) over Map/Seq without the right sort ⇒ Phase 9 picks wrong SMT sort. Fix: carry the operand Type (from Phase 2) into Pred::Cmp; do not infer sort from term shape.
  • <==> non-associativity: lowering a <==> b <==> c requires the parser's associativity (Phase 1) to be respected; mis-grouping silently changes truth value. Debug: golden-file the Imp nesting.

15.7 Performance Concerns

  • Translation is O(size(Expr)) single pass; target < 5 µs per 100-node Expr, no allocation beyond the result tree (reuse Box/Vec with reserved capacity).
  • ctx.bound is a small Vec (typical depth ≤ 3); de-Bruijn lookup is linear in nesting depth — acceptable since quantifier nesting is shallow. Avoid a HashMap per node (allocation churn dominates at corpus scale).
  • Trigger validation walks each trigger once; cache the body's free-var set (computed once per quantifier) to make coverage check O(triggers) not O(triggers × body).
  • Side-channel obligations is a Vec<(Span, Pred)>; pre-size to expected div/mod count to avoid reallocs on large method bodies.

15.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty quantifier body domain (forall i:Int :: true) → VcFormula::True after Phase 8 simplification; translation still emits the Forall faithfully.
  • old(literal) / old(c) for constant c ⇒ state tag irrelevant; must equal its non-old form (idempotent on state-insensitive terms).
  • Quantifier with no trigger: legal; emit empty trigger list, defer to solver auto-triggering (Phase 9 may synthesize). Distinct from an invalid trigger (rejected).
  • Shadowing: forall x:Int :: forall x:Int :: x>0 — inner x shadows outer; de-Bruijn Bound(0) correctly binds inner. Phase 2 may warn; translation must still be capture-free.
  • Mixed-type equality that passed Phase 2 only if same type — so Int == Bool cannot reach here; translator may debug_assert! operand-type equality as a soundness tripwire.
  • Deeply nested old() and quantifiers combined (ensures forall i :: a[i] == old(a)[i]): old applies to a (the map), i stays current/bound — verifies state tags are applied at the right subterm, not the whole expression.

15.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Consequence Mitigation
Missing old() tag (treats pre as cur) old_two_state fixture, AST-walk assert Unsound: postcondition over wrong state can falsely verify Property test: every Old node flips exactly its free vars to Pre
Div/mod side-cond dropped div_obl count assertion Unsound: /0 never proven safe DivOk count == syntactic op count, checked in CI
Quantifier capture (name leaks into Var) de-Bruijn invariant test (no Var name ∈ bound) Wrong VC, possibly unsound de-Bruijn Bound makes capture structurally impossible; assert no overlap
Trigger reordering/loss trigger 1:1 count + structure golden Incompleteness (solver can't instantiate) — not unsoundness Preserve trigger list verbatim; golden file
<==> mis-grouping imp_iff golden Soundness or completeness, depending Respect parser associativity; golden-file nesting

Soundness-critical (must never silently mistranslate): old() state tagging, div/mod side-conditions, equality sorts, division semantics. Completeness-affecting (degrade gracefully): triggers, quantifier auto-instantiation.

15.10 Operational Notes

  • spv print-vc --expr-only <file> dumps the translated VcFormula for the first method's ensures — the debug surface for this phase; stable pretty-printer is the golden-test oracle.
  • RUST_LOG=spv_vc::translate=trace logs each Expr→LExpr node with its StateTag; spv_vc::trigger=debug logs trigger acceptance/rejection with span.
  • Feature flag --strict-triggers (default on): reject interpreted-only / non-covering triggers; off ⇒ warn-only (for differential testing against tools with laxer rules).
  • LExpr/Pred are immutable after construction (shared Box); never mutate in place — Phase 8 simplification produces new trees. This invariant is asserted by #[non_exhaustive]-free pub enums with no interior mutability.
  • Pretty-printer must be reversible enough to diff golden files; pin operator precedence in the printer to match the parser so round-trip diffs are minimal.

16. Phase 4 — IVL core and straight-line desugaring

Establishes the spv-ivl guarded-command IR — the pivot between the typed surface AST and the wp calculus. This phase lowers loop-free, call-free method bodies; contracts (Phase 5), loops (Phase 6), and wp (Phase 7) build on the IvlCmd tree and Reason tags defined here.

16.1 Objectives

  • Define IvlCmd = {Assume, Assert, Havoc, Assign, Seq, Choice} — the LOCKED six-constructor guarded-command core; immutable after construction.
  • Define Reason { kind: VErrorKind, span: Span } — every Assert carries why it exists + source span, so a later Sat model maps to the right VError.
  • Lower straight-line Stmt: Assign(LValue,Expr)→IvlCmd::Assign; Assert(Expr)→Assert(_, Reason{AssertionMightNotHold}); Assume(Expr)→Assume; VarDecl→optional Assign; Return→Seq([]) (contracts deferred).
  • Lower If(g, thn, els)→Choice([assume g; thn], [assume !g; els]) — nondeterministic choice with guarding assumes; both arms desugared recursively.
  • desugar_method(m: &Method) -> IvlCmd body skeleton: produce the loop-free/call-free body's IvlCmd; pre/post wrapping is a no-op stub here (Phase 5 fills it).
  • Invariant: lowering is semantics-preserving — {P} stmt {Q} holds iff {P} desugar(stmt) {Q} holds — verified later via wp; structurally, no Stmt path may drop an Assert.

16.2 Detailed Implementation Steps (ADR-05: Boogie-like six-constructor IVL, not direct AST→VC)

Crate spv-ivl (no_std + alloc), depends on spv-ast. Modules/files:

File Contents
spv-ivl/src/lib.rs re-exports IvlCmd, Reason, desugar_method; crate docs
spv-ivl/src/cmd.rs IvlCmd, Reason, Var, smart ctors seq(), choice(), skip()
spv-ivl/src/desugar.rs Desugarer, desugar_method, desugar_block, desugar_stmt
spv-ivl/src/display.rs Display/pretty-printer for print-ivl (S-expr-ish)
Type defs (from spine; LOCKED names): 
pub enum IvlCmd {
    Assume(Expr),
    Assert(Expr, Reason),
    Havoc(Vec<Var>),
    Assign(Var, Expr),
    Seq(Vec<IvlCmd>),
    Choice(Box<IvlCmd>, Box<IvlCmd>),
}
pub struct Reason { pub kind: VErrorKind, pub span: Span }
pub struct Var(pub Atom);   // resolved name; DSA versioning added in Phase 8, not here

Algorithms:

  • desugar_stmt(&mut self, s: &Stmt) -> IvlCmd — exhaustive match; no _ wildcard arm (forces compile error when a Stmt variant is added).
    • Assign(lv, e)→IvlCmd::Assign(var_of(lv), e.clone()); only LValue::Var legal this phase, Field → defer/unimplemented guard.
    • VarDecl(a, _ty, Some(e))→Assign(Var(a), e); VarDecl(_, _, None)→skip() (Seq([])).
    • Assert(e)→Assert(e, Reason{AssertionMightNotHold, e.span}).
    • Assume(e)→Assume(e).
    • If(g, thn, els)→Choice(box seq([Assume(g), desugar_block(thn)]), box seq([Assume(neg(g)), desugar_block(els)])).
    • While{..} / Call(..)→explicit unreachable!/todo panic guard tagged "Phase 6/5" (scope excludes them; never silently skip).
    • Return→skip() this phase.
  • desugar_block(b)→Seq(b.stmts.map(desugar_stmt)); flatten nested singleton Seq via seq() smart ctor.
  • neg(g) builds Expr::Un(UnOp::Not, box g.clone()) — pushed into IVL as a guard Assume, NOT simplified (simplification is Phase 8).
  • desugar_method(m): match &m.body { Some(blk) => desugar_block(blk), None => skip() }; wrapping = identity stub.
  • Smart ctors: seq(v) drops inner Seq([]), collapses 1-elt; choice(a,b) boxes; keep Reason.span intact through clones.

16.3 Deliverable

Compilable spv-ivl such that any loop-free, call-free typed Method (from Phase 2 output) lowers via desugar_method to a fully-formed IvlCmd tree. spv print-ivl <file.spv> pretty-prints the tree for such methods; each lowered Assert shows its VErrorKind tag.

16.4 Acceptance Criteria

# Criterion Measure
A1 Straight-line method lowers desugar_method returns Ok-shaped tree, no panic
A2 If → exactly one Choice with two guarded arms structural assert in unit test
A3 Assert count preserved #Assert(_) in IVL == #assert in source (no drops)
A4 Reason fidelity every IVL Assert span == originating Stmt::Assert span
A5 Determinism lowering same input twice yields byte-identical print-ivl
A6 Exhaustiveness adding an Stmt variant fails to compile (no _ arm)
A7 Out-of-scope guard While/Call body → explicit panic, never silent skip

16.5 Conformance Fixtures

Dir spv-ivl/tests/fixtures/; each .spv + golden .ivl (pretty-printed). cargo test diffs print-ivl vs golden.

Fixture Source shape Expected IVL
assign_chain.spv x:=1; y:=x+1 Seq[Assign(x,1), Assign(y,x+1)]
assert_assume.spv assume x>0; assert x>=0 Seq[Assume(x>0), Assert(x>=0, AssertionMightNotHold)]
if_then_else.spv if x>0 {y:=1} else {y:=2} Choice(Seq[Assume(x>0),Assign(y,1)], Seq[Assume(!(x>0)),Assign(y,2)])
if_empty_else.spv if g {s} else {} Choice(Seq[Assume(g),..], Seq[Assume(!g)]) (empty arm = bare Assume)
nested_if.spv if a { if b {..} } nested Choice inside guarded arm
vardecl_init.spv var z:Int:=5 Assign(z,5)
Malformed has_loop.spv (while ...) print-ivl exits nonzero, msg "loop lowering: Phase 6"
Malformed has_call.spv (Call(..)) nonzero, msg "call lowering: Phase 5"
Malformed field_lvalue.spv (o.f := e) nonzero, msg "field LValue: Phase 12"

16.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause Debug
If arm drops the guard forgot Assume(g)/Assume(!g) prefix → both arms always feasible → unsound (false "verified") check each Choice arm head is an Assume; assert in test A2
else-less if desugared as one-armed missing empty-els handling → path where !g unconstrained always emit two arms; empty els → Seq[Assume(!g)]
Lost Reason.span on clone building Assert with a synthetic/zero span → counterexample points at wrong line later propagate e.span into Reason; fixture A4
neg(g) double-negates / simplifies "optimizing" !!g here changes semantics surface, fights Phase 8 keep guards literal; no folding in spv-ivl
Silent skip of While/Call a _ => catch-all arm → body verifies vacuously → gravest soundness hole forbid wildcard; explicit panic guards (A6/A7)
Seq over-flattening reorders aggressive flatten that merges across Choice boundary seq() only collapses adjacent Seq/skips, never crosses Choice

16.7 Performance Concerns

  • Lowering is O(n) in AST nodes; single pass, no fixpoint. Target: 10k-stmt method < 5 ms.
  • Choice boxes each arm: heap allocs = #if/2; acceptable. Seq uses Vec (one alloc per block).
  • Expr cloned into IVL (immutable IR) — clone cost ∝ expr size; fine pre-DSA. Avoid deep Box chains by reusing Arc<Expr> only if profiling shows pressure (defer).
  • Tree size ≈ #stmts + 2·#if (extra Assume guards); no blowup here — exponential risk is wp/Choice in Phase 7–8, not lowering.

16.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty method body (Block{stmts:[]}) → skip() (Seq([])); must still wp to true later.
  • if with empty then and empty else → Choice(Seq[Assume(g)], Seq[Assume(!g)]) — pure case split, no effects.
  • Back-to-back assert; assert → two Assert cmds, each own Reason/span (not merged).
  • assume false → kept literally (Assume(False-lit)) — makes downstream path vacuous; correct, not an error.
  • Shadowing handled upstream (Phase 2 resolution) → Var(Atom) already unique; this phase does NOT re-resolve.
  • Guard expr with side-effect-free Call (pure fn) allowed in Assume; impure call as guard is impossible (calls are statements).

16.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Class Detection
Dropped Assert soundness (false verified) A3 assert-count invariant; fuzz diff IVL-asserts vs source-asserts
Mis-guarded Choice soundness A2 structural check + later wp diff vs hand-computed VC
Wrong span in Reason usability (bad counterexample loc) A4 span equality; Phase 11 e2e check
Panic on in-scope input crash / robustness fuzz loop-free/call-free corpus → zero panics
Out-of-scope NOT guarded latent soundness once Phase 6 lands A6/A7 panic-guard tests
Soundness is cardinal: a single dropped/mis-guarded assertion silently verifies a buggy program. Invariants A2/A3 + the no-wildcard match are the front-line defenses; all are machine-checked, never reviewed by eye alone.

16.10 Operational Notes

  • spv print-ivl <file> (added to spv-cli) is the observability surface; stable S-expr format → goldenable + greppable.
  • Pretty-printer prints Reason.kind inline on each Assert (e.g. assert[AssertionMightNotHold] x>=0) for traceability.
  • RUST_LOG=spv_ivl=debug traces each Stmt→IvlCmd lowering step (span + variant).
  • No solver/IO dependency — this phase is pure transform; fully deterministic, hermetic, CI-stable.
  • Versioning note: Var here is the resolved name only; DSA renaming (x@0,x@1) is Phase 8 — do not version in spv-ivl or fixtures will churn.

17. Phase 5 — Procedure contracts and modular verification

17.1 Objectives

  • Make desugar_method wrap a body as assume(requires) ; body ; assert(ensures) (each ensures its own Assert with a distinct Reason{PostconditionMightNotHold}) — close the open end left by Phase 4 (which produced only the straight-line body IvlCmd).
  • Lower Stmt::Call(outs, callee, args) to the modular triple assert(callee.requires[args]) ; havoc(callee.modifies ∪ outs) ; assume(callee.ensures[args, old↦pre, ret↦outs]) — caller sees the callee CONTRACT, never its body. This is what makes verification scale O(methods), not O(call-graph paths).
  • Frame all unmodified state: variables NOT in modifies ∪ outs keep their pre-call values across the call (no havoc, no fresh version) — frame condition is implicit (the un-havoced var is untouched), made explicit only later under DSA (Phase 8).
  • Snapshot old() at method entry and at call sites; substitute formal params/returns by actual args/out-LValues in both the precondition (asserted) and postcondition (assumed).
  • Detect direct/mutual recursion in the call graph and verify modularly WITHOUT inlining (no termination check yet — that is Phase 6 decreases).

17.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Crate / file Item Work
spv-ivl/src/desugar.rs desugar_method(m: &Method) -> IvlCmd finalize: Seq([assume_pre, snapshot_old, <body>, assert_post]); one Assert(e, Reason{PostconditionMightNotHold, e.span}) per m.spec.ensures entry
spv-ivl/src/desugar.rs lower_call(outs,callee,args,&ctx) -> IvlCmd NEW; emits Seq([assert_pre*, Havoc(targets), assume_post*])
spv-ivl/src/contract.rs struct ContractEnv { sigs: Map<Atom, MethodSig> } NEW; per-method MethodSig{params, returns, requires, ensures, modifies} resolved from typed AST
spv-ivl/src/contract.rs MethodSig::frame_targets(outs) -> Vec<Var> modifies lvalue roots ∪ out-param Vars; dedup
spv-ivl/src/subst.rs subst(e:&Expr, σ:&Map<Atom,Expr>) -> Expr capture-avoiding formal→actual substitution over Expr (quantifier-bound vars shadow σ)
spv-ivl/src/subst.rs bind_old(e:&Expr, snap:&Map<Atom,Var>) -> Expr rewrite Old(Var x) ↦ snapshot Var x@pre; only legal in callee ensures (TypeChecker guaranteed)
spv-ivl/src/desugar.rs emit_old_snapshot(vars) -> Vec<IvlCmd> for each x read under old(): Assign(x@pre, x) capturing entry value
  • Call lowering algorithm (ADR-10, P5): (1) look up MethodSig; (2) build σ = {formal_i ↦ args_i}; (3) for each requires r: emit Assert(subst(r,σ), Reason{PreconditionOfCallMightNotHold, call.span}); (4) targets = frame_targets(outs); emit Havoc(targets); (5) σ' = σ ∪ {return_j ↦ LValue→Var(outs_j)}; for each ensures p: emit Assume(subst(bind_old(p, callsnap), σ')).
  • old() at the call's post is the caller's PRE-CALL state of the callee's modified vars → snapshot those into x@callpre Vars BEFORE the Havoc, reference them in the assumed ensures.
  • Order is invariant-critical: assert pre (in old state) → havoc → assume post (post may mention old() = pre-havoc snapshot). Havocing before asserting pre would lose the actual-arg values.
  • Recursion: ContractEnv is built for ALL methods first (two-pass), so a call resolves its callee MethodSig even for self/forward/mutual recursion; no fixpoint, no inlining.

17.3 Deliverable

desugar_method produces a complete IvlCmd for any method whose body contains calls (still no loops): pre/post-wrapped body with each Call replaced by its assert-pre / havoc-modifies / assume-post triple. spv print-ivl method.spv shows the contract triple at each call site. Cross-method verification is structurally enabled (VC for one method depends only on callee SIGNATURES).

17.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • A caller invoking a correct callee whose precondition the caller establishes → IVL contains exactly the asserts/havoc/assume of the contract, NO callee body commands (assert by AST node count: callee statements absent).
  • Callee with K-element modifies and M out-params → Havoc has |unique(K∪M)| Vars; every other in-scope var omitted (frame check via diff against pre-call var set).
  • ensures count → equal count of distinct Assert nodes at method tail, each carrying PostconditionMightNotHold; requires count at call → equal PreconditionOfCallMightNotHold asserts.
  • old(x) in callee ensures resolves to the pre-Havoc snapshot Var, verified by name match in printed IVL.
  • Determinism: desugar_method byte-identical across runs (stable snapshot/var naming); golden print-ivl diff = 0.

17.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Content Expected
call_basic.spv caller calls Inc(x) returns(y) ensures y==x+1 IVL: assert true; havoc y; assume y==x+1; no body
call_pre_fail.spv caller violates callee requires x>0 print-ivl shows Assert(x>0, PreconditionOfCallMightNotHold); verify → that VError
call_modifies_frame.spv callee modifies g; caller asserts h unchanged h NOT in Havoc; assert on h provable
call_old_in_post.spv callee ensures g==old(g)+1 snapshot g@callpre before havoc; assumed g==g@callpre+1
recursion_fact.spv Fact calls Fact resolves via ContractEnv; no inlining; IVL finite
mutual_ab.spv A calls B, B calls A both desugar; each uses other's contract
multi_ensures.spv 3 ensures, 2 requires-at-call 3 tail asserts + 2 call asserts
bad_arity_call.spv (malformed) call passes 2 args to 1-param method rejected upstream in Phase 2 (ArityMismatch); desugar never runs
havoc_out_only.spv out-param not in modifies Havoc includes out Var (assigned by call)

17.6 Pitfalls

  • Inlining the callee body instead of its contract → verification cost explodes and recursion diverges. Root cause: confusing modular VC with whole-program. Debug: print-ivl must show ZERO callee statements; assert callee-body-node-count==0 at each call.
  • Havoc before assert-pre → precondition checked against arbitrary (havoced) actuals → false PASS (unsound). Root cause: wrong command order. Debug: golden IVL ordering test; pre-assert must precede havoc.
  • Forgetting out-params in frame_targets → call result not havoced → caller "knows" a stale value the contract never guarantees → unsound. Debug: diff havoc set vs modifies ∪ outs; fixture havoc_out_only.spv.
  • old() snapshot taken after havoc (or not at all) → assumed postcondition references wrong state, weakening or falsifying frame reasoning. Debug: snapshot Vars must be assigned upstream of Havoc; check call_old_in_post.spv.
  • Variable capture in subst (actual arg mentions a name bound by a callee quantifier) → wrong formula. Root cause: naive substitution. Debug: capture-avoiding subst; fixture with quantified ensures + shadowing actual.
  • Over-framing: havocing a variable NOT in the modifies set → loses a true fact, false FAIL (incompleteness, not unsoundness, but a usability bug). Debug: havoc set ⊆ modifies ∪ outs exactly.

17.7 Performance Concerns

  • Per-call IVL growth = O(|requires| + |ensures| + |modifies∪outs|); independent of callee body size → verification is O(Σ method sizes), the scaling win.
  • ContractEnv built once per Program: O(methods) with hashmap lookup O(1) per call site; no recomputation per call.
  • subst is O(|ensures expr|) per clause; memoize the resolved MethodSig (params/returns/clauses) to avoid re-walking the typed AST per call.
  • Snapshot Assigns add |old-read vars| commands per method/call — bounded, collapsed later by DSA (Phase 8) so they cost ~0 in the final VC.

17.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty requires/ensures → assume(true)/no tail assert (or assert true); empty modifies → Havoc([]) (still havoc out-params).
  • Self-call where callee == caller: same MethodSig, contract used (NOT current-method facts) — no special-casing.
  • Call with zero out-params (Stmt::Call([], m, args)): havoc only modifies.
  • Multiple out-params to multiple LValues: σ' maps each return_j to its outs_j; arity already checked (Phase 2).
  • old() over a non-modified var: snapshot equals current value → assumed equality is trivially carried (no soundness issue, slight redundancy).
  • Method with body: None (abstract/extern): NOT desugared into a VC; still contributes a MethodSig to ContractEnv for callers.

17.9 Failure Analysis

Symptom Likely cause Soundness impact
Buggy callee, caller still verifies callee body inlined OR contract trusted but callee unverified-elsewhere none here (callee verified separately); inlining bug = unsound
Caller verifies despite unmet pre havoc-before-assert ordering UNSOUND — gravest
Stale value survives call out-param/modifies missing from havoc UNSOUND
Correct call reported FAIL over-framing (extra havoc) or wrong old() snapshot incomplete (false alarm)
Recursion never terminates desugaring accidental inlining of recursive callee tool hang, not unsoundness

Soundness is the cardinal property: any path that lets a caller assume more than the callee CONTRACT guarantees (or skip its precondition) is the gravest defect — guard with the IVL-ordering and havoc-set fixtures.

17.10 Operational Notes

  • spv print-ivl --calls method.spv annotates each call's triple with its Reason{kind,span} for inspection.
  • ContractEnv resolution is a verification-time concern only; no SMT yet (VC built in Phase 7, discharged Phase 10).
  • Stable naming scheme for snapshot Vars (x@pre, g@callpre<site-id>) is documented and frozen here — counterexample reconstruction (Phase 11) maps these back to source vars.
  • Logging: at --verbose, emit per-call [contract] callee=<name> requires=<n> ensures=<m> havoc=<vars> to validate framing without reading full IVL.

18. Phase 6 — Loops via the invariant cut (and termination)

Lower while to a loop-invariant cut in spv-ivl (no unrolling); add termination via decreases (well-founded variant). Builds on Phase 5 (desugar_method, contracts, framing). Output is still pure IvlCmd; no wp (Phase 7) yet.

18.1 Objectives

  • Stmt::While { guard, invariants, decreases, body } → IvlCmd by the invariant cut: prove inv on entry, preserved by one arbitrary iteration, and inv ∧ ¬guard reaching exit — loop body verified once, not unrolled.
  • Compute loop targets = variables assigned in body (incl. nested While/Call out-params); havoc exactly those so the inductive step reasons over an arbitrary mid-loop state.
  • Encode partial correctness (inv entry + maintenance + exit) and, when decreases present, total correctness (variant ⪰ 0 and strictly decreases per iteration → no infinite loop).
  • Tag every synthesized Assert with Reason{kind, span}: LoopInvariantOnEntry, LoopInvariantNotMaintained, MightNotTerminate (decreases bound < 0, decreases not-decreasing).
  • Surface a weak invariant as the maintenance assert failing (false ce vs user's invariant, program may be correct) — the classic usability pitfall, mapped distinctly from program bugs.

18.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

  • File spv-ivl/src/loops.rs; desugar_stmt (from Phase 4) dispatches Stmt::While → desugar_while(&While, ctx) -> IvlCmd. desugar.rs re-exports.

  • Loop-target analysis loop_targets(body: &Block) -> Vec<Var>: walk Stmt::{Assign, Call out-LValues, VarDecl, While}, union assigned Vars, dedup, deterministic sort. LValue base var for Field/index assigns. Nested loops/calls fold in transitively.

  • Cut schema (ADR-08): Seq([ assert_all(inv, LoopInvariantOnEntry); Havoc(targets); assume_all(inv); Choice(step, exit) ]):

    step = Seq([ Assume(guard); body_ivl;
             assert_all(inv, LoopInvariantNotMaintained); Assume(false) ])
exit = Assume(¬guard)        // continue with code after the loop

    Assume(false) cuts the inductive branch (it never reaches loop-exit; its job ends at the maintenance assert). assert_all/assume_all fold a Vec<Expr> into per-clause Assert(e, Reason{..,e.span}) / Assume(e).

  • Termination (ADR-09; when decreases non-empty) inside step, snapshot variant after assume inv ∧ guard, before body_ivl:

    Assign(v0_i, decreases[i])           // i = 0..k ; fresh Var per component
… body_ivl …
Assert(rank_lt(decreases, [v0_0..v0_k]), MightNotTerminate)   // new < old
Assert(rank_lo(decreases), MightNotTerminate)                 // bound below

    rank_lt = lexicographic strict decrease over the decreases tuple; rank_lo = each Int component ⪰ 0 (well-foundedness floor). Lexicographic so multi-decreases loops (nested counters) admit one component rising iff a higher one drops.

  • Old() / two-state: loop body has no old(); method old() (Phase 5) refers to method entry, unaffected by havoc targets — emit Assign(old_x, x) only at method entry, never per-loop.

  • Var minting via Phase-4 FreshGen (monotonic counter, names v0_<n>); DSA versioning of targets deferred to Phase 8 passify.

  • Reason.kind ∈ {LoopInvariantOnEntry, LoopInvariantNotMaintained, MightNotTerminate} (all from locked VErrorKind). No new enum variants.

18.3 Deliverable

desugar_while integrated into desugar_method; spv print-ivl method.spv emits the cut for any single loop. A correct loop (e.g. sum-to-n with inv: 0 ≤ i ≤ n ∧ s = i*(i-1)/2, decreases n - i) lowers to a finite, unrolling-free IvlCmd; structurally validated (well-formed Seq/Choice/Assume(false), targets havoc'd) by spv-ivl tests. No wp/SMT yet.

18.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • loop_targets over a 3-deep nested loop returns the exact assigned-var set (golden); idempotent, order-stable.
  • Emitted cut for a single loop has exactly one LoopInvariantOnEntry assert per invariant clause, one LoopInvariantNotMaintained per clause in step, and Assume(false) terminating step.
  • With decreases present: step contains rank_lt + rank_lo asserts tagged MightNotTerminate; absent → neither emitted (partial correctness).
  • No loop unrolling: IVL node count is O(|body|), independent of any literal bound; a loop with bound 10⁹ and bound 2 produce byte-identical IVL.
  • Determinism: same input → identical Var ids and IVL across runs (fixed FreshGen seed).

18.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
loop_sum_ok.spv sum-to-n, full inv + decreases n-i cut w/ entry+maintenance+rank_lt/rank_lo asserts; targets {i,s}
loop_weak_inv.spv inv omits s = … well-formed IVL; maintenance assert present (will FAIL at VC)
loop_no_decreases.spv omit decreases partial-correctness cut; no MightNotTerminate asserts
loop_nested.spv loop in loop, inner assigns j loop_targets outer = {i,j,s}; two nested cuts
loop_lex_decreases.spv decreases x, y rank_lt = lexicographic over (x,y)
loop_old_misuse.spv (malformed) old(i) inside body invariant rejected upstream by Phase 2 OldOutsideEnsures; desugar never runs
loop_bad_decreases.spv (malformed) decreases non-Int/non-well-founded type Phase 2 Mismatch; not lowered

Golden IVL pretty-prints stored under spv-ivl/tests/golden/.

18.6 Pitfalls

  • Under-approx targets → forgotten havoc leaves a stale value, body re-proves inv from concrete state ⇒ unsound loop (verifies buggy code). Root cause: missing nested-loop/call-out var. Debug: diff loop_targets vs body-assign set; assert equality in tests.
  • Over-approx targets → havoc'ing read-only vars weakens inv, spurious maintenance failure (false ce). Debug: confirm var actually appears as assign LValue.
  • Missing Assume(false) in step → inductive branch flows into exit, soundly-modeling-wrong control ⇒ unsound. Symptom: post provable from one iteration. Debug: structural assert on step tail.
  • Variant snapshot misplaced (before assume guard, or after body) → wrong "old" value, termination always trivially passes/fails. Debug: golden order of Assign(v0_i,…) before body_ivl.
  • Strict vs non-strict rank (≤ instead of <) → accepts non-decreasing variant ⇒ non-terminating loop "terminates". Debug: rank_lt must be < per lexicographic step.
  • Floor on non-Int variant (Seq/Map) → ill-typed ⪰ 0; well-foundedness needs a measure. Out of scope here → require Int decreases; reject in Phase 2.

18.7 Performance Concerns

  • IVL size linear in source loop body (one cut, no unrolling); target ≤ ~12 IVL nodes overhead per loop + body size.
  • loop_targets single bottom-up pass, O(|body|); cache per-loop to avoid re-walk in nested loops.
  • Lexicographic rank_lt builds a formula of size O(k²) for k components (k typically ≤ 3) — negligible; do not expand for k = 1.
  • havoc of N targets = one Havoc(Vec<Var>) node, not N nodes — keeps VC quantifier count low downstream (Phase 7 wp(havoc x,Q)=∀x.Q).

18.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty body loop (while g {}) → targets ∅, Havoc([]), cut still valid; inv must be guard-independent.
  • No invariant (invariants: []) → assert_all/assume_all fold to True/no-op; loop havocs targets, assumes nothing ⇒ usually post fails (expected; weak-spec).
  • break/continue — not in locked Stmt; non-goal. Document as unsupported.
  • Guard with side effects / calls — guard is Expr (pure, calls are pure Function), no effect; method Call only via Stmt::Call.
  • Constant-false guard while false {…} → exit branch dominates; body still checked for inv maintenance (vacuously reachable under havoc).
  • decreases referencing a non-target (loop-constant) → never decreases ⇒ MightNotTerminate fires (correct: such a variant cannot bound iterations).

18.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Class Detection Mitigation
Dropped/under-approx havoc target Soundness (gravest) property test: every body-assigned var ∈ targets structural invariant + fuzz nested loops
Missing Assume(false) in step Soundness golden step tail check template-built step, never hand-assembled
Non-strict / absent variant check Soundness (termination) fixture loop_no_decreases vs loop_sum_ok rank_lt strict; MightNotTerminate tag asserted
Over-approx targets Completeness (false ce) loop_weak_inv vs correct-inv diff targets = assigns only
Weak inv reported as program bug Usability Reason.kind = LoopInvariantNotMaintained distinct from AssertionMightNotHold dedicated VErrorKind; Phase 11 message "invariant might not be maintained"

18.10 Operational Notes

  • spv print-ivl --loops file.spv dumps the cut with Reason tags inline for inspection.
  • Soundness is the cardinal property here: a missing havoc target or dropped Assume(false) silently verifies buggy loops — guard with structural + property tests (Phase 13 fuzzes desugar_while).
  • decreases optional ⇒ default partial correctness; document that loops without decreases are not proven terminating (no MightNotTerminate emitted, not a pass).
  • Forward refs: Phase 7 wp consumes this IvlCmd; Phase 8 passify versions havoc'd targets into DSA; Phase 11 maps the failing maintenance/termination assert span → source loop + Counterexample.

19. Phase 7 — Weakest-precondition VC generation

spv-vc. Implement wp(c: &IvlCmd, post: VcFormula) -> VcFormula over the five IvlCmd constructors and emit one VcFormula per method. Method VC = wp(desugar_method(m), VcFormula::True); pre/post wrapping already in the desugared IvlCmd (Phase 5). VC valid iff method meets spec; checked by hand on small inputs this phase (SMT discharge is Phase 9–10). Passive/DSA + simplification deferred to Phase 8 — here wp runs on the raw desugared command and may be exponential; bounded by tiny fixtures.

19.1 Objectives

  • Implement the wp calculus: assume, assert, havoc, assign, Seq, Choice → exact rules below; total coverage of IvlCmd (exhaustive match, no _ arm).
  • Build per-method VC: pub fn method_vc(m: &Method) -> VcFormula = wp(&desugar_method(m), VcFormula::True); correctness ⇔ validity.
  • assert's Reason{kind,span} survives into the produced VcFormula (carried on the conjoined Atom/sub-formula) so Phase 11 can map a Sat model back to a VErrorKind+Span.
  • Substitution Q[e/x] on VcFormula/Expr that is capture-avoiding under Quant/Forall/Exists and treats old() two-state names as opaque.
  • Soundness-first: dropping/weakening any assert conjunct ⇒ false "verified". wp must conjoin every assert. No simplification that can change validity.

19.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Files under crates/spv-vc/src/: lib.rs, formula.rs (VcFormula, Pred), wp.rs (the calculus), subst.rs (capture-avoiding substitution + freshening), expr_to_formula.rs (reuse Phase 3 Expr→Pred/VcFormula bridge). passify.rs is a Phase 8 stub here.

The five rules (LOCKED, ADR-06, P3, paper P5 Dijkstra 1975), implemented in wp.rs:

IvlCmd wp(c, Q) Note
Assume(p) Imp(⟦p⟧, Q) p ⇒ Q
Assert(p, r) And([⟦p⟧, Q]) p ∧ Q; tag ⟦p⟧ with r: Reason
Havoc(xs) Forall(xs×type, [], Q) ∀ x. Q; types from typed AST
Assign(x, e) subst(Q, x, ⟦e⟧) Q[e/x] — only exponential pre-DSA
Seq([c1..cn]) right-fold: wp(c1, wp(c2, … wp(cn, Q))) empty Seq ⇒ Q
Choice(c1, c2) And([wp(c1,Q), wp(c2,Q)]) wp(c1,Q) ∧ wp(c2,Q)
  • ⟦·⟧ = Phase 3 Expr→VcFormula/Pred (expr_to_formula); Assign substitutes into Pred leaves via subst::subst_term.
  • Signatures: pub fn wp(c:&IvlCmd, post:VcFormula)->VcFormula; fn subst(f:&VcFormula, x:&Var, e:&Term)->VcFormula; fn fv(f:&VcFormula)->BTreeSet<Var>; fn freshen(v:&Var, avoid:&BTreeSet<Var>)->Var.
  • Reason propagation: wrap the assert predicate as Atom(Pred::Tagged(reason, inner)) (or side-table Vec<(FormulaId, Reason)>) so the failing conjunct is recoverable; never drop on simplify.
  • Havoc quantifier var types looked up from typed AST (Var carries resolved Type); empty Havoc ⇒ Q unchanged.
  • method_vc orchestration: desugar_method(m) already = assume(requires); body'; assert(ensures, …); just fold wp(.., True). No extra wrapping here.

19.3 Deliverable

spv-vc::method_vc returns a VcFormula per method; spv print-vc <file.spv> (CLI subcommand) pretty-prints it. On the Phase-0 smoke pair: assert true ⇒ VC ≡ True (valid); assert false ⇒ VC ≡ False (invalid). cargo test -p spv-vc green; wp total over all six IvlCmd variants.

19.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • All five+Seq rules implemented exactly; match on IvlCmd exhaustive (compile-checked, no wildcard).
  • wp(assert true, True) → simplifies/equals True; wp(assert false, True) → False; wp(assume p, True) → True (vacuous).
  • Substitution capture-avoiding: wp(x:=y, ∀y. P(x,y)) does not capture y (α-renames bound y) — unit-tested.
  • Hand-checkable: requires x>0; ensures result>0; result:=x ⇒ VC ≡ x>0 ⇒ x>0, valid by inspection.
  • For 6 golden fixtures, printed VcFormula byte-matches expected/*.vc. wp on a loop-free, call-free 30-LOC method finishes <5 ms.

19.5 Conformance Fixtures

crates/spv-vc/tests/fixtures/, each *.spv + *.vc expected (canonical pretty-print):

Fixture Source gist Expected VC (≡) Valid?
assert_true.spv assert true True yes
assert_false.spv assert false False no
assume_imp.spv assume x>0; assert x>=0 x>0 ⇒ (x>=0 ∧ True) yes
assign_subst.spv x:=x+1; assert x>0 (x+1)>0 ∧ True no (x=-1)
choice_branch.spv if c {x:=1}else{x:=2}; assert x>0 (c⇒1>0) ∧ (¬c⇒2>0)-shaped yes
capture.spv x:=y; assert (∀y. x!=y+1) α-renamed, no capture n/a hand-check

Malformed/negative cases: Assign whose RHS references a havoc'd-then-unbound var (must still type as substitution, not panic); empty Seq([]) ⇒ Q; nested Choice depth 4 (VC = 16-way conjunction, asserts no arm dropped); Assert with Reason ⇒ tag present in output (snapshot includes [reason: AssertionMightNotHold@span]).

19.6 Pitfalls

  1. Variable capture in Assign/Havoc: Q[e/x] where e contains a name bound by a Quant in Q ⇒ silent capture ⇒ unsound (false verified). Root cause: naive textual subst. Fix: freshen bound vars whose name ∈ fv(e) before descending. Debug: print-vc after a 1-line ∀-fixture; eyeball binder names.
  2. Seq fold direction: must be right fold (wp(c1, wp(c2,Q))); a left fold computes wp(c2, wp(c1,Q)) ⇒ wrong order ⇒ wrong VC, often still looks plausible. Debug: 2-stmt fixture x:=1; assert x==1 must be valid; reversed gives 1==1 only by luck — use x:=1; assert x==old?-style asymmetric test.
  3. Dropping an assert conjunct on simplify (e.g. And([True, P])→P is fine, but And([P,Q])→P): catastrophic, hides bugs. Root cause: over-eager simplifier in wp path. Fix: do no validity-changing rewrite in Phase 7 (defer to Phase 8); only sound True/False absorption.
  4. wp(assert p,Q)=p∧Q vs p⇒Q: swapping assert/assume rules ⇒ assertions become assumptions ⇒ everything "verifies". Classic. Guard with assert_false.spv ⇒ must be invalid; if it's valid, the rules are swapped.
  5. Exponential Q[e/x] blowup pre-DSA: deeply duplicated Q on chained assigns/Choice ⇒ multi-MB VC, minutes. Expected this phase; cap fixtures small, document; real fix is Phase 8 passify.
  6. old() mishandled in subst: substituting into a two-state Old(e) term must treat the old-state name as opaque (don't rewrite x inside old(x) when assigning current x). Root cause: forgetting two-state. Debug: a method touching old() in ensures.

19.7 Performance Concerns

  • Naive Assign/Choice ⇒ VC size O(2^d) in nesting depth d (each Choice doubles, each Assign duplicates Q). Accepted pre-Phase-8; bound fixtures to d≤6.
  • Targets this phase (no DSA): loop/call-free ≤30-LOC method → VC ≤2 KB, wp <5 ms. Pathological capture/nested-Choice ≤50 ms.
  • Use Box/Rc<VcFormula> to share Q sub-trees where substitution doesn't touch them (cheap structural sharing) — mitigates but doesn't fix exponential; full fix = DSA.
  • fv/subst memoize per node; avoid recomputing free-var sets on every binder.

19.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty body method (body: None or Seq([])): VC = wp(assume requires; assert ensures, True) = requires ⇒ ensures — pure spec implication.
  • Havoc([]) ⇒ Q (no quantifier). Choice with one trivial branch (assume false) ⇒ that conjunct = True.
  • Method with no requires ⇒ assume true prefix ⇒ True ⇒ Q ≡ Q. No ensures ⇒ trailing assert true ⇒ ∧ True.
  • Nested Quant in ensures substituted by an Assign of a captured name (the capture.spv case).
  • assert false reachable only on an infeasible path (assume false; assert false) ⇒ VC False ⇒ False∧True ≡ True, valid (dead code).

19.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Symptom Cause Detection / mitigation
Capture random fixture verifies that shouldn't non-capture-avoiding subst capture.spv snapshot; fuzz binder names (Phase 13)
Rule swap assert false "verifies" assume/assert rules transposed assert_false.spv gate; assert VC≡False
Conjunct loss buggy program passes simplify drops assert structural eq vs expected/*.vc; forbid rewrites here
Fold order asymmetric asserts wrong left fold of Seq order-sensitive fixture
Exponential wp hangs/OOM on medium input pre-DSA duplication size cap + watchdog; punt to Phase 8
old() corruption two-state ensures wrong subst into Old term dedicated old() fixture

Soundness rule: any divergence between hand-computed VC and emitted VC on the 6 goldens blocks the phase.

19.10 Operational Notes

  • spv print-vc <file> (+--method <name>, --pretty/--sexp) for inspection; deterministic output (sorted conjuncts, stable var ids) so snapshots are byte-stable.
  • RUST_LOG=spv_vc::wp=trace logs each rule application + intermediate Q size (watch for doubling).
  • Pretty-printer shares the VcFormula AST printer with Phase 8/9; Reason tags rendered inline [k@span].
  • No SMT here: validity is established by hand / snapshot only. Discharge wiring is Phase 9–10; do not call any SmtSolver.
  • Property test (proptest): wp(c, True) is total (never panics) on randomly generated well-typed IvlCmd; substitution preserves fv modulo the substituted var.

20. Phase 8 — Passive form (DSA) and VC simplification

Crate: spv-vc. Inserts passify between desugar/contract-wrapping (Phases 5–6) and wp (Phase 7). Closes the exponential-VC hole: naive wp(x:=e, Q)=Q[e/x] + wp(c1[]c2,Q)=wp(c1,Q) AND wp(c2,Q) duplicates Q per assignment and per branch → blowup.
