Fix = Flanagan-Saxe passive form (DSA) + a simplify pass.

20.1 Objectives

  • DSA transform passify(c: &IvlCmd) -> IvlCmd: each Var assigned at most once; Assign(x,e) → Assume(x_k == e) over fresh versions x_k; Choice arms reconciled by joining versions at merge points so wp over passive form is linear-ish (no Q duplication on assign, shared Q across branches).
  • After DSA, IvlCmd contains no Assign (only Assume/Assert/Havoc/Seq/Choice) → wp uses only the duplication-free rules.
  • simplify(f: &VcFormula) -> VcFormula: constant-fold, flatten nested And/Or/Imp, drop True/False units, dedup → shrink VC before SMT.
  • Invariant: passive form is semantics-preserving w.r.t. wp — valid(wp(passify(c), Q)) == valid(wp(c, Q)) (soundness AND completeness of the transform; a DSA bug = false "verified" = gravest failure).
  • Target: VC size O(n) to O(n log n) in command count n; eliminate the 2^k growth on k sequential assigns / nested choices.

20.2 Detailed Implementation Steps (ADR-07, paper P1 Flanagan & Saxe 2001)

Files under spv-vc/src/:

File Contents
dsa.rs passify, VersionMap, fresh_version, branch reconcile/join
simplify.rs simplify, fold_const, flatten, Smart-constructors
var.rs Var versioning: Var{ base: Atom, gen: u32 } (LOCKED Var extended with gen)
lib.rs re-export; passify + simplify wired into per-method VC build
Signatures (illustrative bodies ≤2 lines): 
pub struct VersionMap(pub HashMap<Atom, u32>);          // base -> current generation
fn fresh(vm: &mut VersionMap, x: &Atom) -> Var;          // bump gen, return Var{x, gen}
fn cur(vm: &VersionMap, x: &Atom) -> Var;                // read current Var (no bump)
pub fn passify(c: &IvlCmd) -> IvlCmd;                     // top: passify_rec(c, &mut VersionMap::new())
fn passify_rec(c: &IvlCmd, vm: &mut VersionMap) -> IvlCmd;
fn reconcile(a: VersionMap, b: VersionMap) -> (VersionMap, IvlCmd, IvlCmd); // join + per-arm Assume tails

DSA recursion (the contract):

  • Assign(x,e): e' = substitute(e, vm) (rewrite each Var to its current version); x_new = fresh(vm,x); emit Assume(x_new == e'). No Q duplication: assignment now flows through an equality assume, never Q[e/x].
  • Havoc(xs): for each x, fresh(vm,x) (bump version, no equality) → models nondeterministic re-assignment; subsequent reads see the fresh version.
  • Assume(p)/Assert(p,r): rewrite p via vm (substitute current versions); preserve Reason r verbatim (error mapping intact).
  • Seq([c1..cn]): thread one mutable vm left→right; collect passive cmds in order.
  • Choice(a,b): clone vm → vm_a, vm_b; passify each arm independently; reconcile: for every base whose gen differs across arms, pick gen_join = max+1 (fresh merge var x_join), append to each arm Assume(x_join == x_armgen); set vm[x]=gen_join. Result Choice(Seq[a'; joinTail_a], Seq[b'; joinTail_b]). simplify rewrite rules (apply to fixpoint, bottom-up):
    Rule Rewrite
    const fold And([..,False,..])→False; Or([..,True,..])→True; Imp(True,Q)→Q; Imp(_,True)→True; Imp(False,_)→True; Not(True)→False/Not(False)→True; Not(Not(p))→p
    unit drop remove True from And, False from Or
    flatten And([..,And(xs),..])→And([..xs..]); same for Or; right-nested Imp left intact (curry preserved)
    dedup structural-eq dedup within And/Or children (hash by structural key)
    empties And([])→True; Or([])→False; singleton And([p])→p
    Smart constructors mk_and, mk_or, mk_imp, mk_not apply rules at build time (avoid building garbage then GC-ing). wp (Phase 7) uses smart constructors → simplification is partly fused into generation.

20.3 Deliverable

passify produces Assign-free passive IvlCmd; wp(passify(desugar(m)), True) yields a VC whose node count is linear-ish in the program. spv print-vc --passify --stats file.spv prints the VC and a size table (nodes, depth, #foralls). A size benchmark (benches/vc_size.rs) shows k sequential assigns → naive VC >= 2^k nodes vs passive VC O(k).

20.4 Acceptance Criteria

# Criterion Measure
A1 Assign-free passify(c) contains zero IvlCmd::Assign (debug assert + test walks tree)
A2 Linear size chain of k=20 assigns: passive VC nodes <= 6k; naive (reference impl, test-only) >= 2^k
A3 Branch sharing j nested Choice: passive VC nodes O(j) not O(2^j); j=15 finishes <50ms
A4 Semantics preserved for a fixture corpus, Unsat(NOT wp(passify(c),True)) == Unsat(NOT wp_naive(c,True)) (both via SMT)
A5 Reason intact every Assert Reason{kind,span} in passive form equals the pre-DSA Reason (no error-mapping drift)
A6 Simplify sound random VcFormula → simplify is logically equivalent (truth-table check on ≤8 atoms)

20.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
dsa/chain20.spv (x:=x+1 ×20 then assert) passive VC nodes <= 120; reported by --stats; verifies
dsa/swap.spv (t:=x; x:=y; y:=t) three Assume(==) over x_1,y_1,t_1; post x==old y holds
dsa/branch_join.spv (if g {x:=1} else {x:=2}; assert x>=1) join var x_2; both arms Assume(x_2==x_1); verifies
dsa/nested_choice15.spv 15-deep Choice; passive nodes O(15); --stats proves no 2^15
dsa/havoc_read.spv (havoc x; assert x==x) fresh x_1; trivially verifies (reflexivity), no equality assume for the havoc
simplify/folds.spv VC with True/False literals collapses to a single atom; --stats depth <=2
Malformed: feed an IvlCmd still containing Assign to wp debug build panics assign in passive form; release: caught by A1 test
Malformed: reconcile over arm with un-versioned base internal error unjoined var <base>; regression test guards

20.6 Pitfalls

  • Missing branch join → arms diverge in versions; reads after Choice pick the wrong arm's gen; VC is silently wrong → unsound. Root cause: forgetting reconcile for bases touched in only one arm. Debug: assert vm_a and vm_b agree on gen for every base after join; diff version maps in print-ivl --passify.
  • Stale read across Seq — substituting e with a post-bump version (read after writing x in same Assign): must compute e' BEFORE fresh(vm,x). Symptom: x:=x+1 becomes x_1==x_1+1 (False) → spurious "verifies/fails". Order is load-bearing.
  • Havoc emits equality — havoc must only bump gen, never Assume(x_new==…); a stray equality re-pins the havoc'd var → loses nondeterminism → unsound loop/call framing. Debug: count Assume after passify; havoc adds none.
  • old() two-state version capture — Old(e) reads the pre-state version (gen 0 / entry snapshot), NOT the current vm gen. Pin old versions at method entry; substituting them with current versions breaks postconditions. Cross-check Phase 5 contract wrapping.
  • Smart-ctor over-flatten of Imp — flattening Imp (right-assoc) into And corrupts wp's assume-rule shape. Only And/Or flatten; Imp stays curried.
  • Structural dedup hashing quantifiers — dedup must respect bound-variable α-equivalence or skip Forall/Exists children; naive hash drops distinct quantified facts → unsound simplify. Restrict dedup to atoms/connectives.

20.7 Performance Concerns

  • passify: single pass, O(n) cmds; substitute over expr is O(|e|); total O(total expr size). VersionMap = HashMap<Atom,u32>; clone per Choice arm is O(#live bases) (small) — acceptable; intern Atom so keys are u32.
  • VC node target: passive <= 6n nodes for n cmds; naive blows to 2^k. Benchmark gate: chain20 passive <150 nodes, build <5ms.
  • simplify: bottom-up to fixpoint, but rules are size-non-increasing → terminates in O(node); cap iterations at 3 (rules confluent in practice). Smart ctors avoid a separate pass.
  • Memory: passive IvlCmd is O(n); avoid building naive VC ever (no transient 2^k) — fuse smart ctors into wp.

20.8 Edge Cases

Case Handling
var read before any write cur returns gen 0 (= entry/parameter version); no assume emitted
Assign self-ref x:=x Assume(x_1==x_0); simplify may keep (needed for framing); not dropped as trivial
empty arm Choice(Seq[],b) empty arm contributes only join tail; reconcile against vm unchanged side
both arms havoc same x each bumps independently; join picks max+1, both arms assume x_join==x_armgen
Havoc of var never read after still bump (cheap); dead but sound; simplify leaves (no equality to fold)
simplify(True) / already-minimal VC identity (fixpoint reached in 0 rewrites)
deeply nested And from wp of long Seq flatten to one And([..]); depth O(1) not O(n)

20.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Symptom Detection / Guard
Dropped join (unsound) buggy branch program reports Verified A4 differential vs wp_naive; soundness fixtures from Phase 13
Version-order bug x:=x+1 always verifies/always fails wrongly dsa/chain20 + swap golden VCs; substitute-before-fresh unit test
Exponential leak OOM / timeout on nested_choice15 A3 size assert; --stats node count regression in CI
Reason loss counterexample points at wrong assert/span A5 Reason-equality test; round-trip span through passify
Unsound simplify (quantifier dedup) valid VC simplified to weaker formula → false verify A6 truth-table on connectives; quantifiers excluded from dedup
old mis-versioning postcondition spuriously holds/fails two-state fixtures shared with Phase 5; pin-at-entry assertion

20.10 Operational Notes

  • CLI: spv print-vc --passify (default on for verify), --no-passify (debug, may explode — warn), --stats (node/depth/forall counts), --simplify/--no-simplify.
  • Debug dumps: print-ivl --passify shows DSA cmds with x@gen rendering; diff against non-passive to inspect version threading.
  • RUST_LOG=spv_vc::dsa=debug logs each fresh/reconcile; gated behind --verbose.
  • Determinism: version numbering is left-to-right, reconcile is max+1 → byte-stable VCs across runs (required for golden fixtures + differential diffing).
  • Invariant enforced in verify path: debug_assert!(no_assign(&passive)) before handing to wp; release relies on A1 test coverage. DSA correctness is on the soundness-critical path — any change here re-runs the full Phase 13 PASS/FAIL corpus before merge.

21. Phase 9 — SMT encoding and SMT-LIB emission

Crate: spv-smt. Input: simplified DSA VcFormula (Phase 8). Output: SmtTerm tree + a well-formed SMT-LIB 2 script + the SmtSolver/SmtResult/Model interface (definitions only; discharge is Phase 10). Bridge stage: pure first-order logic → solver-input syntax. Soundness depends on encoding each VcFormula/Pred operator to its exact SMT semantics — a mis-encode here = false "verified".

21.1 Objectives

  • Total map VcFormula -> SmtTerm over sorts SmtSort = {Int, Bool, Uninterp(Atom), Array(k,v)}; spv-ast::Type -> SmtSort (Int->Int, Bool->Bool, Ref->Uninterp("Ref"), Map(k,v)->Array, Seq(t)->Array(Int,t), Named(n)->Uninterp(n)).
  • Emit SMT-LIB 2.6 text: set-logic AUFNIRA (default), sort/fun decls, one assert, no check-sat from encoder (driver adds it). Round-trips through Z3/cvc5/#278 unchanged.
  • Attach quantifier Trigger patterns from Forall to SMT-LIB (! body :pattern (...)); never emit a triggerless universal that the matching loop can't fire.
  • Define trait SmtSolver { fn check(&mut self, assertions: &[SmtTerm]) -> SmtResult }, enum SmtResult { Unsat, Sat(Model), Unknown }, struct Model (var->value), without a real backend (Phase 10).
  • Invariant: encoding is semantics-preserving — VcFormula valid ⟺ SmtTerm valid; assert the negation at the driver, so verify = prove NOT-VC Unsat.

21.2 Detailed Implementation Steps (ADR-16 trigger annotation; papers P9 de Moura–Bjørner Z3 and P10 trigger selection)

Files under spv-smt/src/ (see §10 repo layout):

File Contents
sort.rs SmtSort; pub fn lower_type(t: &Type) -> SmtSort; sort interning
term.rs enum SmtTerm (below); SmtFun builtin enum; constructors
encode.rs pub fn encode_formula(f: &VcFormula, env: &mut Env) -> SmtTerm; encode_pred(p: &Pred); encode_term for arith/old lowered terms
decls.rs Env symbol table: gather every free Var+Type, every uninterpreted fn (Pred::App), into (declare-const)/(declare-fun)/(declare-sort)
trigger.rs pub fn annotate_triggers(q: &VcFormula) -> Vec<SmtPattern>; trigger inference for trigger-less quantifiers (subterm heuristic)
smtlib.rs pub fn emit(script: &SmtScript) -> String; S-expr pretty-printer (Display), name mangling
solver.rs trait SmtSolver, enum SmtResult, struct Model, enum ModelValue, struct NullSolver (always Unknown, for tests)

SmtTerm shape (sorted FOL + quantifiers + triggers):

pub enum SmtTerm {
  True, False,
  Const(Symbol, SmtSort),                 // declared var
  App(SmtFun, Vec<SmtTerm>),              // and/or/not/=>/=/+/-/*/div/mod/</<=/ite/select/store
  Uf(Symbol, Vec<SmtTerm>, SmtSort),      // uninterpreted-fn application (pure fn / pred)
  IntLit(i128), BoolLit(bool),
  Forall(Vec<(Symbol, SmtSort)>, Vec<SmtPattern>, Box<SmtTerm>),
  Exists(Vec<(Symbol, SmtSort)>, Box<SmtTerm>),
}
pub struct SmtPattern(pub Vec<SmtTerm>);  // multi-pattern :pattern

Algorithm encode_formula (structural, total): True/False->True/False; And/Or->n-ary App; Not->App(Not,..); Imp->App(Imp,..); Forall(vs,trigs,b)-> SmtTerm::Forall(map vs, encode trigs, encode b); Exists-> existential (no triggers); Atom(Pred)->encode_pred. Pred cases: comparisons </<=/=, boolean atoms, pure-fn/predicate application -> Uf, map read -> select, map write -> store. Int arithmetic = unbounded mathematical Int (no machine wrap) — matches source Int semantics; document as locked.

Env pass (pre-encode): walk VcFormula, collect (a) free vars -> declare-const name sort; (b) uninterpreted symbols (each Pred::App head, each old-introduced two-state heap symbol) -> declare-fun; (c) sorts Uninterp(n) -> declare-sort n 0. Deterministic emission order (sorted by symbol) -> stable golden output.

SmtScript = { logic: &str, sorts, funs, consts, assertion: SmtTerm }. Encoder builds it from VC + Env; emit renders. Bool-vs-Int discipline: SmtTerm is sort-checked at construction (debug_assert arg sorts); a Bool used where Int expected = encoder bug, caught early.

21.3 Deliverable

spv-cli print-smt <file> prints a complete, standalone-parseable SMT-LIB 2 script for the VC of each method: header (set-logic AUFNIRA), all declare-sort/const/fun, exactly one (assert <encoded VC or its negation>), optional :named per top-level conjunct. Piping the script (with a trailing (check-sat)) into z3 -smt2 -in or cvc5 parses and returns sat/unsat with no syntax error. NullSolver lets spv-verify link without a backend.

21.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Every Phase-7/8 VC over the supported Types emits with zero SMT-LIB parse errors across Z3 4.12+ and cvc5 1.x (validated by z3 parse / cvc5 --parse-only).
  • encode_formula is total: no VcFormula/Pred/Type variant panics or hits unreachable!; property test over random formulas = 0 panics in 100k cases.
  • Sort soundness: 0 sort-mismatch errors reported by the solver's type checker on the full conformance corpus.
  • Triggers: 100% of emitted Forall carry ≥1 :pattern; inferred patterns contain only universally-bound subterms (no free-var-only triggers).
  • Golden stability: byte-identical print-smt output across runs (deterministic symbol order); diff-clean under repeated invocation.

21.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected print-smt / behavior
enc_int_add.spv (assert (not (=> ... (= (+ x 1) y)))); Int sort; unsat when valid
enc_bool_imp.spv nested (=> .. (and ..)); no Int decls
enc_map_select.spv Map<Int,Int> -> (Array Int Int); (select m i) present
enc_seq.spv Seq<Int> -> (Array Int Int); length via Uf len
enc_forall_trigger.spv (forall ((i Int)) (! (=> ..) :pattern ((f i))))
enc_forall_notrigger.spv inferred :pattern injected; warn-once on stderr
enc_uf_purefn.spv (declare-fun f (Int) Int); application as Uf
enc_ref_field.spv (declare-sort Ref 0); heap as (Array Ref Int)
enc_old_twostate.spv distinct heap0/heap const decls, both used
enc_negation.spv driver-mode asserts (not VC); encoder-mode asserts VC
malformed enc_bad_sort.vc construction debug_assert trips: Bool arg to + -> internal error, never emitted
malformed enc_unbound.vc free var absent from Env -> hard error UnboundSymbol, not silent emit

Each *.spv pairs with *.smt2.golden; CI diffs print-smt against golden and z3 --parse-only each golden.

21.6 Pitfalls

  • Bool/Int sort confusion: source Bin(And,..) on Ints vs + on Bools. Root cause: lossy Type->SmtSort. Debug: sort-tag every SmtTerm, debug_assert at App construction; bisect by print-vc vs print-smt.
  • Triggerless or over-broad triggers -> matching loop never fires (false Unknown) or fires forever (timeout/MBQI blowup). Root cause: trigger inference picks a term covering too few/too many bound vars. Debug: z3 smt.qi.profile=true, inspect instantiation count.
  • Unbound symbol emitted (Var not in Env) -> solver "unknown constant" parse error, or worse, solver treats it as fresh and returns spurious Sat. Root cause: Env pass missed a var introduced by DSA renaming/skolem. Debug: assert encode only references declared symbols.
  • Polarity / negation drift: asserting VC instead of NOT VC (or double-negating) inverts every result — bugs "verify", correct code "fails". Root cause: confusing encoder-mode vs driver-mode. Debug: a known-unsat fixture must come back unsat only when negated.
  • Int overflow in literals: source Lit beyond i128 truncates. Root cause: SMT Int is unbounded but encoder host type isn't. Debug: emit big literals as decimal strings, not host ints.
  • Non-deterministic decl order -> golden churn, flaky diffs. Root cause: iterating a HashMap. Fix: BTreeMap/sorted keys.

21.7 Performance Concerns

  • VC size: target methods ≤ a few k IVL cmds -> SmtTerm ≤ ~10^4–10^5 nodes; encode in O(VC nodes) single pass, no quadratic re-walks.
  • Hash-cons SmtTerm (intern identical subterms) so DSA-shared subformulas don't duplicate; cuts emission size and string length materially on Choice-heavy VCs.
  • emit streams into a preallocated String (write!), no intermediate Vec<String> joins; target < 50 ms to render a 10^5-node script.
  • Trigger inference is local (per-quantifier subterm scan), O(body size); never global fixpoint.
  • Keep declarations minimal: only symbols actually referenced -> smaller solver input, faster parse.

21.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty VC (True) -> (assert true) (negated: (assert (not true)) = trivially unsat -> verified).
  • Forall with empty body trigger / nested quantifiers -> patterns scoped to innermost binders only.
  • Exists -> no :pattern (triggers are for universals); skolemization is the solver's job, not the encoder's.
  • Nested Map<Map<..>,..> / Seq<Seq<..>> -> nested (Array .. (Array ..)); recursion-depth-safe lowering.
  • Ref equality + null -> null as a declared Ref const, = over Ref sort.
  • Same source var name in two scopes -> already alpha-renamed by DSA; encoder mangles Var (name+version) into a unique SMT Symbol, never colliding.
  • Reserved SMT-LIB keywords as source identifiers (e.g. assert, let) -> mangle/quote (|sym|).

21.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Severity Detection
Operator mis-encoded (e.g. - as mod) catastrophic (false verified) differential VC eval / Phase 13 expected-FAIL corpus
Dropped conjunct during encode catastrophic golden diff + assertion-count check
Wrong sort, solver type error high (no result) solver parse stage; --parse-only CI
Trigger too tight -> Unknown medium (incompleteness, sound) timeout/Unknown rate metric (Phase 10)
Trigger too loose -> timeout medium QI profile, instantiation cap
Polarity inverted catastrophic known-unsat sentinel fixture

Cardinal rule: any encoding bug that could make a buggy program emit an unsat-yielding script is a soundness defect — gate on the expected-FAIL corpus (Phase 13), never trust "it parses".

21.10 Operational Notes

  • spv-smt is no_std+alloc for the SmtTerm/encode core; emit returns String (alloc), solver I/O lives behind the trait (std side in Phase 10).
  • Logic selection: default AUFNIRA; downgrade to QF_*/AUFLIA when a VC has no quantifiers/nonlinear arith (faster) — pick per-VC from a feature scan.
  • --smt-dump <dir> writes each method's .smt2 for offline replay against any solver (debugging, bug reports, differential).
  • Emitter version-pins SMT-LIB 2.6; document any solver-specific extension (e.g. Z3 :weight) as opt-in, never in the portable golden.
  • Determinism (sorted decls, hash-consing) is mandatory for reproducible CI goldens and for diffing solver-input across verifier versions.

22. Phase 10 — SMT backend integration and result interpretation

Wires a concrete SmtSolver behind the trait, discharges check(NOT VC) per method, maps Unsat => Verified / Sat(Model) => VError / Unknown => honest-failure, and drives whole-program verdicts via incremental push/pop. Phase 9 produced SmtTerm + SMT-LIB text + the trait; this phase makes the trait run. Counterexample text (Model -> source vars) is Phase 11; here Model is captured verbatim, not yet decoded.

22.1 Objectives

  • Discharge VC: assert NOT VC -> solver; Unsat => Verified, Sat(model) => Failed, Unknown => reported, NEVER Verified (soundness invariant).
  • Implement two SmtSolver impls: Z3Process (pipe SMT-LIB to z3 -in) and Smt278 (in-proc #278 link); both behind the locked trait, selected by CLI/env.
  • Per-method incremental solving: (push 1) ... (check-sat) [(get-model)] (pop 1) reusing one solver session across all methods of a Program.
  • Whole-program driver in spv-verify: build IVL -> wp -> passify -> simplify -> encode -> check, fold per-method results into VerifyResult, set process exit code.
  • Resource governance: per-method timeout (--timeout-ms, default 10000) + rlimit; Unknown(timeout) distinguished from Unknown(incomplete); partial verdict on remaining methods.

22.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Crates/files (all under locked spine — extend, do not rename):

File Adds
spv-smt/src/solver.rs SmtResult { Unsat, Sat(Model), Unknown }; UnknownReason { Timeout, Incomplete, ResourceOut, SolverError } attached to Unknown; Model { raw: String, assigns: Vec<(Atom, Value)> } (assigns empty until P11)
spv-smt/src/z3_process.rs pub struct Z3Process { child, stdin, stdout, opts }; spawns z3 -in -smt2; impl SmtSolver for Z3Process
spv-smt/src/smt278.rs pub struct Smt278 { ctx }; in-proc handle to #278 solver lib; impl SmtSolver for Smt278 (feature smt278)
spv-smt/src/session.rs SolverSession wrapping a dyn SmtSolver + a stable prelude (sort/fn decls + axioms) asserted once at depth 0
spv-verify/src/driver.rs pub fn verify_program(p:&Program, s:&mut dyn SmtSolver) -> VerifyResult; verify_method(...)
spv-cli/src/main.rs verify flags `--solver z3

Trait (LOCKED, ADR-12) — fn check(&mut self, assertions: &[SmtTerm]) -> SmtResult;. Phase-10 protocol layered atop it via SolverSession:

pub fn discharge(s:&mut SolverSession, vc:&VcFormula) -> SmtResult; // asserts NOT(encode(vc))
  • Encode-negation: discharge calls spv-smt::encode(&VcFormula::Not(box vc)) -> Vec<SmtTerm> (prelude decls + one assertion). Validity(VC) == Unsat(NOT VC). Do not negate twice — single Not wrapper, then encode.
  • Process backend protocol (Z3Process): emit (set-option :produce-models true) (set-option :timeout <ms>) once; per method (push 1), emit method-local decls + (assert <neg-vc>), (check-sat), read one of sat|unsat|unknown; if sat -> (get-model) read till balanced ); if unknown -> (get-info :reason-unknown) to fill UnknownReason; then (pop 1).
  • Prelude vs per-method scope: program-global symbols (uninterpreted fns, axioms, datatype/map sorts from P3/P12) asserted at depth 0 in SolverSession::new; method-local DSA versions + the negated VC asserted inside the matching push/pop so versions of method A don't leak to method B.
  • In-proc backend (Smt278): translate SmtTerm -> #278 AST via a thin shim; mirror push/pop on #278's assertion stack; map #278's result enum -> SmtResult. Same trait, no SMT-LIB text on the hot path (text only under --dump-smt).
  • Driver fold: verify_program iterates decls, skips Method.body==None (trusted contract), builds VC per method, discharge, classifies; accumulates Vec<VError> (kind+span carried from the Reason of the asserted IvlCmd::Assert that the VC came from). All methods checked even after a failure (no early exit) -> complete report.
  • Verdict mapping (LOCKED, ADR-13): Unsat -> (this method) Verified; Sat(m) -> VError{kind from Reason, span, counterexample: Counterexample{ bindings: vec![], at: span }} (bindings filled P11); Unknown(r) -> VError{kind: same Reason.kind, ...} but tagged inconclusive in render — emitted as "could not prove", exit code 2, never counted as Verified.

22.3 Deliverable

spv verify prog.spv returns end-to-end verdicts for a whole multi-method program: prints verified (exit 0) when every method's NOT VC is Unsat; prints per-method FAILED: <VErrorKind> at <span> (exit 1) on any Sat; prints UNKNOWN: <reason> at <span> (exit 2) on any inconclusive method with no Sat. One persistent solver session per invocation. Both --solver z3 and --solver smt278 produce identical PASS/FAIL verdicts on the fixture suite.

22.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Round-trip: tests/pass/*.spv -> all verified exit 0; tests/fail/*.spv -> FAILED exit 1 with correct VErrorKind+span; tests/unknown/*.spv -> exit 2 (never 0).
  • Soundness gate (cardinal): zero buggy fixture ever exits 0; CI fails build if any tests/fail/ case reports verified.
  • Z3 vs Smt278 verdict-agreement = 100% on PASS/FAIL fixtures (Unknown allowed to differ).
  • Incremental session reused: solver spawned exactly once per verify (assert via process-count / strace-free counter in test harness), not once per method.
  • Timeout honored: a method exceeding --timeout-ms yields Unknown(Timeout) within timeout_ms + 500ms wall, others still checked.

22.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
tests/pass/abs.spv verified, exit 0, one check-sat -> unsat
tests/pass/multi_method.spv 3 methods, all unsat, single session, exit 0
tests/fail/off_by_one.spv FAILED: PostconditionMightNotHold at ensures span, sat, exit 1
tests/fail/bad_assert.spv FAILED: AssertionMightNotHold, exit 1
tests/unknown/nonlinear.spv x*x*x>=0 nonlinear -> UNKNOWN: Incomplete, exit 2
tests/unknown/quant_loop.spv trigger-starved forall -> UNKNOWN: Timeout under --timeout-ms 800
tests/golden/abs.smt2 exact SMT-LIB from --dump-smt, byte-stable
Malformed: z3 binary absent --solver z3 -> SolverError, exit 3, message "solver not found", NOT a verdict
Malformed: solver prints (error "…") parsed -> Unknown(SolverError), exit 3, never Verified
Malformed: truncated model (EOF mid (model) SmtResult::Unknown(SolverError); no panic

22.6 Pitfalls

  • Unknown silently treated as proved -> false verified (gravest). Root cause: matching only Unsat/Sat, defaulting else to pass. Debug: assert match is exhaustive over SmtResult; fuzz-inject unknown responses. Fix: Unknown => non-zero exit, distinct render.
  • Negation forgotten / double-negated: asserting VC not NOT VC flips every verdict (valid VC -> sat). Debug: --dump-smt and eyeball the lone (assert (not …)); unit-test discharge(True)==Unsat, discharge(False)==Sat.
  • Stack desync (push/pop imbalance): a missing pop after Sat+get-model leaks method A's assertions into B -> spurious unsat (false verified) or contradiction. Debug: track depth counter, assert ==0 between methods. Fix: RAII ScopeGuard emitting pop on drop.
  • Reading past the model: get-model output is multi-line; reading a fixed line count truncates -> parse fail or hang. Fix: read until paren-balanced; never line-count.
  • Deadlock on full pipe: large VC fills the OS pipe; if reader blocks before writer flushes, both stall. Fix: write+flush then read; separate stdout drain thread for big dumps.
  • Stale timeout option: :timeout set once but solver resets it after (reset); re-emit per push if needed. Debug: confirm timeout fires on nonlinear.spv.

22.7 Performance Concerns

  • Incremental push/pop reuses learned prelude facts: target ~2-5x speedup vs spawn-per-method on 50-method programs; one process spawn (~30-80ms) amortized.
  • Per-method solve target: < 200ms for loop-free / linear-arith methods; < 2s with quantifiers + triggers; hard cap --timeout-ms (default 10000).
  • SMT-LIB emission off the hot path: text only when --dump-smt; in-proc Smt278 skips serialization (target ~1.5-3x faster term ingest).
  • VC -> SMT term count should stay linear in IVL size (DSA from P8); flag if a single method emits > 10^5 terms (likely passify regression).
  • Whole-program 200 methods: target < 30s total, < 512MB RSS; memory dominated by solver, bound via --rlimit-mem-mb.

22.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty program / all bodies None: verified, exit 0, zero check-sat calls.
  • Method whose VC simplifies to True (P8): skip solver entirely -> trivially Verified (perf + avoids vacuous solver round-trip).
  • VC False (e.g. assert false): must be Sat -> FAILED, exit 1 (sanity that negation isn't inverted).
  • Solver returns sat with empty model (all vars don't-care): still FAILED; Model.assigns empty, P11 handles rendering.
  • Mixed verdicts: methods {unsat, sat, unknown} -> overall exit 1 (Sat dominates Unknown dominates Verified); all three lines printed.
  • Solver emits unsupported for an option: tolerate, continue (non-fatal); unsupported for a logic command: SolverError, exit 3.

22.9 Failure Analysis

  • Solver crash mid-session (SIGSEGV in #278/Z3): check returns Unknown(SolverError); driver marks remaining methods inconclusive (session unusable), exits 3 — never 0.
  • unsat from a contradictory prelude/axiom set: every method "verifies" vacuously (false soundness). Detect via a planted (assert false)-free consistency probe at depth 0: check-sat of prelude alone must be sat; if unsat, abort with "inconsistent axiomatization", exit 3.
  • Verdict divergence Z3 vs Smt278 on a PASS/FAIL case: indicates encoding bug (P9) or backend shim bug; CI diff dumps both .smt2 for triage; treat as build-breaking.
  • Timeout flapping (nondeterministic Unknown): seed solver RNG (:random-seed 0), pin :timeout; flaky fixtures quarantined, not silently passed.
  • Mitigations: exhaustive SmtResult match, paren-balanced reads, RAII pop, prelude consistency probe, differential dual-backend CI, golden .smt2.

22.10 Operational Notes

  • CLI: spv verify f.spv --solver z3|smt278 --timeout-ms N --rlimit-mem-mb M --dump-smt DIR; print-smt (P9) unchanged.
  • Solver discovery: $SPV_Z3 or PATH z3; version logged (z3 --version); supported range pinned in solver.rs.
  • Exit codes (LOCKED contract): 0=all verified, 1=at least one Sat/FAILED, 2=Unknown w/o any Sat, 3=solver/infra error. CI keys off these.
  • Logs: --log-smt traces each push/check-sat/result/pop with method name + wall ms; --dump-smt writes <method>.smt2 for offline replay (z3 method.smt2).
  • Reproducibility: solver :random-seed 0, deterministic decl ordering (sorted by Atom id), byte-stable golden .smt2 in CI.

23. Phase 11 — Counterexample reconstruction and error localization

Sat model from Phase 10 -> source-level VError{kind, span, counterexample}. This phase closes the loop: every NO verdict becomes a localized, re-executable diagnostic. Lives in spv-verify; consumes Model (spv-smt), Reason tags (spv-ivl), DSA versioning side-tables (spv-vc), and source Spans (spv-syntax).

23.1 Objectives

  • Parse Model (var -> Value) from SmtResult::Sat; recover Int/Bool/Ref/Array assignments; no panics on partial/sparse models.
  • Invert DSA: SMT/skolem name x@3 -> source Atom x at the version live at the failing point; recover old(x) (pre-state version x@0) for two-state display.
  • Locate failing assert: walk Reason.span of the assertion whose negation the model satisfies -> exact program point at: Span.
  • Classify VErrorKind from the Reason.kind carried on that assert (8 variants); never default-bucket.
  • Render Counterexample{bindings: Vec<(Atom, Value)>, at: Span} listing only in-scope, source-named variables with concrete values + the failing line.
  • Soundness boundary: a malformed model -> Unknown-style "verification inconclusive", NEVER Verified and NEVER a fabricated pass.

23.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Files (all in spv-verify/src/), driven by the discharge loop from Phase 10:

File Responsibility
cex.rs reconstruct(model, vc_meta) -> Counterexample; orchestrates parse->invert->select->render
model_parse.rs parse_model(raw: &Model) -> ModelEnv (name -> Value), array/lambda lookups
dsa_map.rs DsaSideTable consumed from spv-vc: (Atom, version) <-> Var, point-of-use version map
classify.rs classify(reason: &Reason) -> VErrorKind; pure projection over Reason.kind
render.rs render(VError) -> String human report (frame + bindings + old() column)

Side-table contract (LOCKED data passed forward, not new public types): spv-vc passify already emits fresh Var versions; it must additionally export, per Var, (source: Atom, gen: u32, span: Span) and per assert a back-pointer to its Reason. spv-smt SmtTerm emission must name SMT constants deterministically from Var so inversion is total. wp's VC negation tags each conjunct-as-asserted with the originating Reason (one assert == one potential failure site).

Algorithm — failing-assert selection (ADR-17, P7): VC = wp(desugar(method), true); negation NOT VC is a disjunction over assert sites (wp(assert p,Q)=p AND Q => failure = some p false). For the returned model, evaluate each asserted p under ModelEnv; the first/least-dominated assert evaluating to false is the localized failure. Its Reason{kind, span} yields VErrorKind + at.

Algorithm — DSA inversion: at at, look up the live version gen for each source Atom; read ModelEnv["{atom}@{gen}"]; old(x) -> gen=0. Loop targets after havoc use the havoc-fresh version. Skolem constants from Forall/Exists (quantifier instantiation witnesses) map to fresh Atoms rendered as ?k0, ?k1.

Value recovery: Int = SMT numeral (handle (- n)); Bool = true/false; Ref = Ref(id) opaque tag (no address); Map/Seq (Phase 12 sorts) = array model -> finite [i := v, default] table.

23.3 Deliverable

spv verify bad.spv on any failing program prints a localized report: VErrorKind, source file:line:col of the failing assert/ensures/invariant, and a value table of in-scope variables (+ old() column) that drives the failure — re-substitutable into the source to reproduce. print-smt + a --model dump path exposes the raw model for debugging the inversion.

23.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • 100% of Failed(_) verdicts carry a non-empty at: Span pointing inside the offending Reason.span (no Span::dummy).
  • For every Phase 13 expected-FAIL fixture, VErrorKind matches the annotated expectation (golden file) — 8/8 kinds exercised.
  • Reconstructed bindings are sufficient: re-evaluating the failing assert with those values yields false (self-check assertion in reconstruct, debug builds).
  • Reconstruction latency < 5 ms for VCs up to 10^4 atoms; < 50 ms at 10^5; allocation O(model size), not O(VC size).
  • No unwrap/panic reachable from parse_model on any solver-shaped output (fuzz-verified, 11.6).

23.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected output
cex_assert.spv (assert x > 0 with x havoc'd) AssertionMightNotHold @ assert line; binding x = 0 (or <0)
cex_post.spv (ensures result == x+1, body returns x) PostconditionMightNotHold; x, result, old(x)
cex_callpre.spv (call violates callee requires) PreconditionOfCallMightNotHold @ call site; arg bindings
cex_inv_entry.spv (inv false before loop) LoopInvariantOnEntry @ while
cex_inv_step.spv (weak inv) LoopInvariantNotMaintained @ inv clause; loop-target values post-havoc
cex_divzero.spv (a / b, b nondet) DivisionByZero; b = 0
cex_modifies.spv (writes outside modifies) ModifiesViolation; offending field/var
cex_terminate.spv (decreases not decreased) MightNotTerminate; variant values old vs new
cex_quant.spv (failing forall) skolem witness ?k0 = … shown
cex_partial_model.spv (solver omits a var) binding rendered x = <unconstrained>, still localized
Malformed: cex_garbage_model (corrupt model text) "verification inconclusive (model parse)", verdict NOT Verified
Malformed: cex_no_false_assert (model satisfies all asserts) internal error logged; treated as Unknown, never Verified

23.6 Pitfalls

  • Wrong assert selected — multiple asserts false under the model; root cause: VC short-circuits or dominance order lost. Debug: dump per-assert eval table (--model), pick the syntactically-first dominating false. Picking the wrong one mislocalizes but stays sound.
  • Stale DSA version — reading x@2 when x@3 is live at at => bogus value. Root cause: point-of-use version map not threaded through Choice joins. Debug: assert gen monotonic per path; cross-check against passify side-table.
  • old() collapsed to current — pre-state x@0 aliased to entry version => old(x) column equals x. Root cause: two-state framing dropped in inversion. Detect via cex_post.spv where they must differ.
  • Model-completeness assumption — solver leaves a var unconstrained; reading it unwraps. Root cause: SMT models are partial. Fix: render <unconstrained>; never fail.
  • False counterexample to a weak invariant — LoopInvariantNotMaintained is the user's invariant being too weak, NOT a program bug; must say so in render or users chase phantom bugs (the classic deductive-verification usability trap).
  • Skolem leakage — internal quantifier witnesses (?k) printed as if source vars, confusing users. Root cause: no source-name filter. Fix: segregate skolem bindings under a "witness" sub-list.

23.7 Performance Concerns

  • Inversion is O(model entries); avoid re-walking the full VC per assert — precompute the assert->Reason index once at VC build (Phase 7/8), reuse here.
  • Array/Map model values can be large (lambda/store chains); cap rendered entries (default 32, --cex-array-cap), summarize tail as ….
  • String formatting dominates for big bindings; render lazily, only the selected failing assert's in-scope set, not the whole ModelEnv.
  • Memoize Atom->live-gen lookup at at (single pass over the DSA map), not per-variable scan.

23.8 Edge Cases

  • Vacuous failure: assert under a Choice branch guarded by assume false — branch is dead; must not be selected (its p is unconstrained). Filter unreachable asserts via path feasibility in ModelEnv.
  • Unknown (timeout) -> no model -> emit MightNotTerminate-agnostic "inconclusive", NOT a counterexample; never fabricate bindings.
  • Multiple independent failures in one method: report the first localized VError; Failed(Vec<VError>) may hold more if cheaply available, but one is mandatory.
  • Variable shadowing across scopes resolving to same Atom: disambiguate by Span-scoped Var, not by name.
  • Counterexample with only quantifier witnesses, zero program vars (pure-spec failure): still produce at + witness list.
  • Negative/large Int numerals and SMT (- 5) form; bitwidth-free Int so no overflow.

23.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Severity Consequence Mitigation
Report Verified despite a Sat model CRITICAL (soundness) Buggy program accepted Sat => always Failed; reconstruction failure degrades to "inconclusive", never Verified
Mislocalized at (right NO, wrong line) Medium (usability) Wasted debugging Self-check: chosen assert evaluates false; golden-span fixtures
Wrong VErrorKind Low–Med Misleading category classify is total projection over Reason.kind; 8/8 golden coverage
Fabricated/garbage bindings Medium False reproduction Re-eval self-check (23.4) gates emission in debug; fuzz in release
Panic on partial model Medium Crash instead of diagnostic Total parse_model; fuzz cex_partial_model

Soundness invariant (LOCKED): reconstruction NEVER upgrades a verdict. It only annotates an already-decided Failed. A reconstruction bug can mislead but can never hide a real bug.

23.10 Operational Notes

  • CLI: spv verify prints the human report; --model <path> dumps raw SMT model; --cex-array-cap N bounds array rendering; --no-cex suppresses bindings (verdict only) for CI speed.
  • Exit codes unchanged: Failed => nonzero (set Phase 10); reconstruction does not alter exit status.
  • Logging: RUST_LOG=spv_verify::cex=debug traces assert-selection table + DSA inversion; off by default.
  • Determinism: same model + same source => byte-identical report (sorted bindings by source order then Span); required for golden tests and --diff against Boogie/Dafny (Phase 13).
  • Forward hook: Phase 12 heap/Map and pure-function fuel introduce new model shapes (array stores, fuel-bounded witnesses); model_parse.rs must extend its Value decoder without touching selection/classification.

24. Phase 12 — Heap, arrays, functions, and datatypes

Richer features lift the verifier from scalar (Int/Bool/Ref) reasoning to real programs: a heap/memory model (objects + fields as Map, dynamic-frames framing), arrays/sequences with their defining axioms, user-defined pure functions (uninterpreted symbol + defining axiom, well-founded recursion bounded by fuel), and algebraic datatypes (constructors/selectors/testers). All new state threads through Var/Type/IvlCmd/VcFormula/SmtTerm already built; the heap is itself a single Var of Map-sort. Soundness is the cardinal constraint: every new axiom must be sound, fuel must terminate quantifier instantiation (no matching loops), and modifies/framing over the heap must never let a buggy program verify.

24.1 Objectives

# Objective Done-when
O1 Heap as one Var: Map(Ref, Map(FieldId, Value)) (Boogie two-level select/update) field read/write verifies; null deref → AssertionMightNotHold
O2 Dynamic-frames framing: modifies is a set of Ref; havoc heap, re-assume frame axiom on unmodified (o,f) caller sees unmodified fields preserved across a call
O3 Arrays/Seq with select/store + length/index axioms; Map extensionality Seq get-after-set, length, slice verify
O4 Pure Function → uninterpreted SMT fn + defining Forall axiom; recursion gated by fuel decrement recursive fib/fact verify without diverging
O5 Algebraic datatypes (Decl::Axiom-emitted): constructors, selectors, testers, injectivity/exhaustiveness axioms match/construct over a List/Option datatype verifies
O6 Sound triggers on every new axiom; fuel and frame axioms terminate instantiation benchmark suite (24.5) terminates < per-method budget; zero false "verified"

24.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

spv-ast (types.rs, resolve.rs, typeck.rs) — add the heap & datatype surface:

  • Type::Map(K,V), Type::Seq(T), Type::Ref, Type::Named(Atom) already exist; add a resolver table datatypes: Map<Atom, DatatypeDef> where DatatypeDef { ctors: Vec<Ctor> }, Ctor { name: Atom, fields: Vec<(Atom, Type)> }. Field access Expr::Field(e, f) type-checks against the static field decl of e's Ref/Named type; selector arity/return-type checked.
  • New TypeError reuse: out-of-scope field → Undeclared, ctor arity → ArityMismatch, non-Ref .field → Mismatch.

spv-ivl (heap.rs new, desugar.rs) — model state (ADR-14):

  • Introduce the distinguished heap variable Var::heap() of sort Map(Ref, Map(FieldId, Value)). Lower x := o.f → Assign(x, select(select(heap,o), f)) guarded by Assert(o != null, Reason{DivisionByZero→null-deref reuses AssertionMightNotHold}). Lower o.f := e → Assign(heap, store(heap, o, store(select(heap,o), f, e))).
  • desugar_call: extend the havoc(modifies) step — modifies now denotes a frame (set of Ref/(o,f)); emit Havoc(vec![Var::heap()]) then Assume(frame_axiom): Forall (o:Ref, f:FieldId) [select(select(heap',o),f)] . (o,f) ∉ modifies => select(select(heap',o),f) == select(select(old_heap,o),f).
  • desugar_function: a pure Function emits no IvlCmd; instead spv-vc/spv-smt emit its uninterpreted symbol + defining axiom (below). Calls in Expr stay as Expr::Call.

spv-vc (logic.rs, axioms.rs new) — encode operators & axioms as VcFormula/Pred:

  • Pred gains Select, Store, Len, Index. select(store(m,k,v),k)=v and k≠k' => select(store(m,k,v),k')=select(m,k') are the map axioms; Seq adds len(store_idx(s,i,v))=len(s) and index-after-update.
  • Fuel (ADR-15): a pure recursive fn f becomes uninterpreted F: (Fuel, args...) -> ret. Defining axiom Forall (n:Fuel, x) [F(succ(n),x)] . F(succ(n),x) == body[x; F replaced by F(n,...)]. Call sites use F(succ(succ(zero)), args) (default fuel = 2, configurable). F(zero, x) has no unfolding axiom → recursion bottoms out.
  • Datatype axioms (in axioms.rs): injectivity sel_i(C(x1..xn))=xi, distinctness C != D (testers is_C), exhaustiveness Forall v. (is_C(v) || is_D(v) || ...).

spv-smt (encode.rs) — SmtSort::Array(Box,Box) for Map/heap/Seq; Uninterp(Atom) for Ref, FieldId, Fuel, datatype sorts. Emit native SMT-LIB declare-datatypes for ADTs when the backend supports it, else fall back to the uninterpreted+axiom encoding. Attach triggers (24.6) to every Forall.

24.3 Deliverable

spv verify succeeds on heap-mutating methods, array/seq programs, recursive pure functions (fib, fact, sum), and datatype programs (List, Option); spv print-vc/print-smt show frame axioms, fuel-bounded function axioms, map/seq axioms, and datatype declarations. Counterexamples (Phase 11) still map heap reads back to o.f source form. Zero false "verified" across the 24.5 suite.

24.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
Heap programs verifying (PASS suite) ≥ 30 fixtures, 100% correct verdict
Frame soundness every FAIL fixture mutating outside modifies → ModifiesViolation; 0 false PASS
Recursive-fn termination all recursive-fn VCs return Unsat/Sat in < 5 s; no solver timeout from matching loops
Fuel correctness fib(n)==fib(n-1)+fib(n-2) proved at fuel ≥ 2; fuel = 0 leaves F opaque (provable lemmas only)
Datatype proofs match-exhaustiveness + injectivity programs verify; bad-selector program → typed error
Determinism byte-identical SMT-LIB for the same input across 50 runs (axiom + trigger ordering stable)

24.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expects Notes
heap_field_rw.spv Verified o.f := 5; assert o.f == 5 via select/store
heap_null_deref.spv Failed AssertionMightNotHold read o.f with o possibly null
frame_preserved.spv Verified call mutates o, caller asserts unmodified p.f unchanged
frame_violation.spv Failed ModifiesViolation body writes a field not in modifies
seq_get_set.spv Verified s[i:=v][i]==v, len preserved
fib_recursive.spv Verified recursive pure fn, fuel = 2
mutual_rec.spv Verified even/odd mutual recursion, shared fuel
list_datatype.spv Verified Cons/Nil, head/tail, injectivity
matching_loop.spv Verified (terminates) adversarial axiom; fuel/triggers must prevent divergence
Malformed: bad_selector.spv TypeError::Mismatch selector applied to wrong ctor/type
Malformed: arity_ctor.spv TypeError::ArityMismatch constructor called with wrong field count
Malformed: field_unresolved.spv TypeError::Undeclared .f on type lacking field f

Golden outputs (print-vc, print-smt) checked per fixture; FAIL fixtures assert exact VErrorKind + span.

24.6 Pitfalls

  • Matching loop (axiom f(x) ⇒ f(g(x)) triggers on f(x), instantiates forever): root cause = trigger that produces a fresh term matching itself. Fix = fuel guard makes the trigger term strictly smaller (F(succ(n),..) produces F(n,..)); debug with solver instantiation logging ((set-option :smt.qi.profile true) / #278 quant-stats), cap instantiations.
  • Unsound frame axiom (havoc heap but forget to re-constrain unmodified (o,f)): silently lets a buggy method "verify" because the post can assume anything about untouched state. Detect via frame_preserved.spv/frame_violation.spv differential; the absence of the Forall ... ∉ modifies => == clause is the smoking gun in print-vc.
  • Missing null guard on select(select(heap,o),f): a deref of a non-allocated/null o reads junk and may verify. Root cause = lowering Field without the Assert(o!=null). Symptom: heap_null_deref.spv returns Verified instead of Failed.
  • Over-strong datatype distinctness (asserting C(x)!=D(y) and an over-broad exhaustiveness that omits a ctor): makes the context inconsistent → everything "verifies". Debug: ask solver for Unsat of axioms-alone; if axioms alone are Unsat the theory is broken.
  • Fuel too low → real obligations fail (false counterexample to a correct program): user sees PostconditionMightNotHold though program is correct. Distinguish from a real bug by re-running with higher --fuel (config verify.fuel).
  • Map vs extensional equality: m1 == m2 requires extensionality axiom; without it equal-by-content maps are deemed distinct → spurious failures. Emit (= m1 m2) only with Forall k. select(m1,k)==select(m2,k) available.

24.7 Performance Concerns

  • VC size grows with frame axioms (one nested Forall per call); keep triggers tight so the solver instantiates O(callsites) not O(2^n). Target: VC ≤ 3× the scalar-only Phase 7 size for the same control flow.
  • Fuel default = 2 caps function unfolding; per-method solver time budget 5 s (configurable). Each extra fuel ≈ doubles instantiation depth — never auto-escalate fuel silently.
  • Datatype axioms: prefer native declare-datatypes (solver-internal, fast) over the uninterpreted+axiom fallback, which adds O(ctors²) distinctness terms.
  • Heap select/store nesting (two-level) doubles term depth vs flat arrays; share the heap Var across all field ops to keep DSA (Phase 8) versions linear.

24.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty modifies (pure method): frame axiom degenerates to "heap unchanged" — Forall (o,f). heap'==old_heap; must still emit, not skip.
  • Self-recursive fn with decreases but no fuel-bounded base case → encode termination via the Phase 6 well-founded variant and fuel; the two are orthogonal (fuel bounds instantiation, decreases proves termination).
  • Mutual recursion: shared Fuel decremented across the cycle so even(succ(n)) unfolds to odd(n) (one level), never both at full fuel.
  • Nested datatypes (List<Option<Int>>) and recursive datatypes (List): injectivity/exhaustiveness still finite; size/measure axiom optional, only if a decreases references it.
  • Map with Ref keys aliasing: o == p not assumed; framing must hold under arbitrary aliasing (havoc allows o==p).
  • Quantifier in spec ranging over heap (forall o :: o.f > 0) — trigger on select(select(heap,o),f), bound by allocated-set predicate to stay sound.

24.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Severity Detection Mitigation
Frame axiom dropped/mis-framed → false Verified critical (unsoundness) frame_violation.spv regression; differential vs Dafny subset mandatory frame-axiom assertion in desugar_call unit test; fuzz havoc/modifies sets
Matching loop → solver Unknown/timeout high (completeness) per-method timeout + qi-profile fuel-guarded triggers; instantiation cap; explicit Unknown report (Phase 10)
Inconsistent datatype axioms → vacuous Verified critical axioms-only Unsat check in CI derive ctor/tester/selector axioms mechanically from DatatypeDef, never hand-write
Null-deref unguarded → false Verified critical heap_null_deref.spv assert-o!=null invariant test on every Field lowering
Fuel too low → false Failed low (usability) re-run higher fuel configurable --fuel; emit hint "increase fuel" on PostconditionMightNotHold over recursive fn

Soundness failures (critical) gate the build — any false "verified" in the 24.5 PASS/FAIL suite fails CI per the project soundness contract.

24.10 Operational Notes

  • New config: verify.fuel (default 2), verify.heap_model (maps | dynamic_frames, default maps), surfaced as CLI --fuel N / --heap-model.
  • spv print-smt annotates each axiom with a source comment (; frame axiom for call@span, ; defining axiom F fuel, ; datatype List) for solver-log triage.
  • Telemetry per method: VC size, axiom count, quantifier-instantiation count, solver time, fuel used — logged for the Phase 13 benchmark gate.
  • Backends without declare-datatypes auto-select the uninterpreted+axiom fallback; backend capability probed once at startup via the SmtSolver trait, logged.
  • Reproducibility: axioms emitted in a fixed canonical order (datatypes, then functions by name, then per-call frame axioms by span) so SMT-LIB is byte-stable for golden tests and differential runs.

25. Phase 13 — Validation: soundness suite, differential, fuzzing, benchmarks

The "done" gate. Closes the loop: every prior phase (parse → typecheck → IVL → wp → DSA → SMT → counterexample → heap/funcs) is now stress-tested against the cardinal property — soundness: spv must NEVER report Verified for a buggy program. Completeness (verifying every correct program) is secondary, bounded by SMT, tracked but not gating. No new pipeline behavior; this phase is harness + corpus + gates spanning all seven spv-* crates, driven through spv-verify/spv-cli.

25.1 Objectives

  • Soundness suite — corpus of expected-PASS + expected-FAIL .spv programs; invariant: zero buggy program returns VerifyResult::Verified. A single unsound verify fails the build, full stop.
  • Completeness ledger — separately track expected-PASS programs that fail to verify (Failed/Unknown); regressions warn, not block; known-fail quarantine bucket bounded by SMT incompleteness (quantifiers, nonlinear arith, Unknown).
  • Differential — compare spv verify verdict vs Boogie/Dafny on a shared language subset; verdict mismatch on a PASS↔FAIL boundary = sev-1.
  • Fuzzing — cargo-fuzz targets on Lexer/Parser, desugar_method, wp/passify, VcFormula→SmtTerm emission; zero panics/OOM/hangs for the CI budget.
  • Benchmarks — criterion over VC size, wp term count, SMT solve time vs Phase-7/8/10 targets; regression gates.
  • Artifact — published bench_report.md + conformance.json ledger; make validate green.

25.2 Detailed Implementation Steps (ADR-19 differential oracle, ADR-20 fuzzing, ADR-21 benchmarking)

  • spv-verify test crate tests/conformance/ — corpus under tests/corpus/{pass,fail,known_fail}/*.spv. Each .spv carries a // EXPECT: header line (see 25.5) parsed by tests/conformance/harness.rs::Expectation.

  • Harness API (spv-verify, #[cfg(test)] + a validate lib feature so spv-cli can reuse):

    pub struct Expectation { pub verdict: ExpectVerdict, pub kinds: Vec<VErrorKind>, pub spans: Vec<Span> }
pub enum ExpectVerdict { Pass, Fail, KnownFail }   // Pass=Verified; Fail=Failed(..); KnownFail=quarantine
pub fn run_case(src: &str, exp: &Expectation) -> CaseOutcome;   // drives full spv pipeline
pub enum CaseOutcome { Ok, Unsound{got:VerifyResult}, Incomplete, WrongKind, WrongSpan, SolverUnknown }

  • Soundness check in run_case: parse→typecheck→desugar_method→passify→wp→encode→SmtSolver::check(NOT VC). exp == Fail AND result Verified ⇒ CaseOutcome::Unsound ⇒ test panics with the offending file path (build-breaking).

  • Completeness check: exp == Pass AND result Failed/Unknown ⇒ Incomplete (logged to ledger, non-fatal). Failed on a Fail case additionally asserts VErrorKind + Counterexample.at span match exp.kinds/exp.spans.

  • Ledger writer tests/conformance/ledger.rs → target/conformance.json: {file, expect, outcome, kind, solver_ms, vc_terms} per case; aggregate {pass, fail, known_fail, unsound, incomplete}.

  • Differential tests/differential/ — subset.ebnf defines the Boogie/Dafny-shared fragment (Int/Bool only, no Seq/Map/datatypes, no Old nesting). differential.rs runs spv verify + (feature-gated) external boogie/dafny binary via std::process::Command; compares PASS/FAIL verdict only (not error text). Skips gracefully if external tool absent (SPV_DIFF=1 to require).

  • Fuzz crate fuzz/ (separate, std, cargo-fuzz/libfuzzer-sys): targets fuzz_targets/{parse,desugar,wp,smt_emit}.rs. parse feeds bytes→Parser; desugar/wp/smt_emit feed arbitrary-derived Method/IvlCmd/VcFormula (add #[derive(Arbitrary)] behind fuzzing feature on those types). Each asserts no panic + bounded output size.

  • Benchmarks benches/ (criterion): vc_size.rs (counts VcFormula nodes pre/post passify+simplify), solver_time.rs (per-method check wall-time using the in-tree mock + optional Z3). Baselines in benches/baseline.json; gate via criterion's --save-baseline/threshold.

  • make validate target: cargo test -p spv-verify --features validate + cargo fuzz run <t> -- -max_total_time=$BUDGET (per target) + cargo bench -- --baseline main + ledger/report emit.

25.3 Deliverable

  • Corpus ≥ 150 PASS + 120 FAIL + quarantined known_fail .spv programs across all features (assert/assume, contracts, calls, loops+decreases, heap/Map, Seq, pure functions+fuel, datatypes, old()/two-state).
  • tests/conformance/harness.rs, ledger.rs; tests/differential/; fuzz/ (4 targets); benches/ (2 benches + baseline).
  • make validate green; generated target/conformance.json + checked-in bench_report.md.
  • CI job validate wiring all of the above with explicit fuzz time budget.

25.4 Acceptance Criteria

# Criterion Threshold
A1 Unsound verifications (FAIL case → Verified) 0, build-breaking
A2 PASS-case conformance ≥ 98% Verified; the rest only in known_fail
A3 FAIL-case VErrorKind + span match 100% of Verified=false cases
A4 Differential verdict mismatches on subset 0 (when SPV_DIFF=1)
A5 Fuzz: panics / OOM / hangs over CI budget 0 crashes; budget ≥ 60s/target CI, 15min/target nightly
A6 VC size post-passify vs naive wp linear-ish, ≤ 2× source-stmt count (no exponential)
A7 Per-method median solver time, subset ≤ 200 ms; corpus total cargo test ≤ 90 s
A8 Bench regression vs baseline.json < 10% slowdown / VC-size growth gates CI
A9 bench_report.md + conformance.json present, regenerated, committed

25.5 Conformance Fixtures

// EXPECT: header grammar (one line, first non-blank):

  expect    ::= "// EXPECT:" verdict ( ";" attr )* ;
  verdict   ::= "PASS" | "FAIL" | "KNOWN_FAIL" ;
  attr      ::= "kind=" VErrorKind | "span=" line ":" col | "reason=" text ;
File Header Expected outcome
pass/assert_true.spv // EXPECT: PASS Verified
fail/assert_false.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=AssertionMightNotHold Failed, that kind, span at the assert
fail/post_violated.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=PostconditionMightNotHold counterexample binds offending var
fail/call_pre_unmet.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=PreconditionOfCallMightNotHold at call site span
fail/inv_entry.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=LoopInvariantOnEntry —
fail/inv_step.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=LoopInvariantNotMaintained weak-invariant pitfall case
fail/div_zero.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=DivisionByZero —
fail/modifies.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=ModifiesViolation frames an unlisted var
fail/nonterm.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=MightNotTerminate bad/missing decreases
known_fail/quant_trigger.spv // EXPECT: KNOWN_FAIL; reason=SMT Unknown on trigger quarantined
Malformed (harness self-test)
_meta/no_header.spv none harness errors "missing EXPECT"
_meta/bad_verdict.spv // EXPECT: MAYBE harness errors "unknown verdict"
_meta/unknown_kind.spv // EXPECT: FAIL; kind=Nope harness errors "unknown VErrorKind"

Soundness mutation set: for N random PASS cases auto-inject a bug (negate a requires, drop an ensures conjunct, off-by-one a decreases) → must flip to FAIL; if any still Verified ⇒ A1 breach.

25.6 Pitfalls

  • Unknown masquerading as success — root cause: treating SmtResult::Unknown as "not Sat" ⇒ false Verified. Fix: Unknown is NEVER Verified (Phase-10 invariant); harness asserts it. Debug: grep ledger outcome=SolverUnknown on FAIL cases.
  • Vacuous PASS from contradictory requires — assume(false) makes any postcondition verify; soundness suite can't catch (program genuinely "correct"). Detect via a dedicated pass/_vacuity_* audit: assert requires is SAT before trusting Verified.
  • Differential subset drift — root cause: a .spv uses Seq/Map/datatype outside subset.ebnf ⇒ external tool rejects ⇒ spurious mismatch. Fix: differential.rs pre-filters via a subset linter; CI fails loudly on out-of-subset diff cases.
  • Fuzz arbitrary generates ill-typed AST — wp/desugar assume typechecked input ⇒ panic that is NOT a real bug. Fix: fuzz desugar/wp only on inputs that pass TypeChecker, or guard targets to post-typecheck IR; raw bytes only for parse.
  • Span/kind drift after refactor — A3 brittle to renumbered spans. Fix: fixtures pin line:col, not byte offsets; a span shift fails the case and flags the regression deliberately.
  • Bench nondeterminism from solver — Z3 wall-time varies ⇒ flaky A8. Fix: bench VC-size (deterministic) as the hard gate; solver-time as a soft, median-of-N, warn-only signal.
  • Trigger-loop hang in fuzz smt_emit — bad Trigger annotation ⇒ matching loop in solver, not spv. Fix: fuzz emission asserts well-formedness of triggers only; cap solver via -max_total_time, not unbounded check.

25.7 Performance Concerns

  • Corpus run must stay ≤ 90 s: reuse one SmtSolver session with push/pop per method (Phase-10), not a fresh process per case; parallelize cases across threads (solver instances pinned per worker).
  • Fuzz budget bounded by -max_total_time + -rss_limit_mb=2048; OOM treated as a crash (A5).
  • Bench inputs sized to expose the exponential trap: a chain of if (Choice) of depth 20 — naive wp ⇒ 2^20 terms; passify (DSA) must keep it ≤ ~2× linear (A6). Report both pre/post counts.
  • criterion warm-up + 100 samples on micro VC-size; solver-time bench uses fewer samples (timeout-bounded).

25.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty Block body / method with no body (body: None, contract-only) → trivially Verified; PASS fixture.
  • assert false reachable only on a dead branch (guard false) → must still NOT verify unless provably dead; soundness fixture.
  • Quantifier with no Trigger → solver may return Unknown → known_fail, never silent PASS.
  • decreases on a non-Int (Seq length) variant → total-correctness PASS fixture; missing decreases on a while → MightNotTerminate FAIL.
  • Nested Old(Old(e)) / old() outside ensures → rejected at typecheck (Phase-2), so it's a TypeError fixture, not a verify case.
  • Mutually recursive pure functions with insufficient fuel → Unknown ⇒ known_fail; with adequate fuel ⇒ PASS.
  • Differential: a program PASS in spv but the external tool times out (not FAIL) → recorded as skip, not mismatch.

25.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Mitigation
Unsound verify (dropped assert, mis-framed Havoc, op mis-encode) A1, mutation set catastrophic — tool is worthless build-breaking gate; root-cause to the exact wp/encode rule
False FAIL (sound but incomplete) A2 ledger Incomplete usability quarantine known_fail; file SMT issue
Differential mismatch A4 trust vs reference bisect to operator/encoding; sev-1
Fuzz panic in wp/desugar A5 robustness on adversarial input minimize crash, add as regression fixture
Bench regression A8 scalability erosion (exp. blowup creeping back) block CI; inspect passify output term count
Flaky solver Unknown ledger churn noisy CI bump timeout, add trigger, or quarantine — never relax soundness

25.10 Operational Notes

  • make validate = the project done-gate: conformance green + unsound=0 + fuzz-clean (budget) + bench within gates.
  • CI: PR runs 60 s/target fuzz + VC-size gate; nightly runs 15 min/target fuzz + Z3 differential (SPV_DIFF=1). Fuzz corpus persisted as a CI artifact and reseeded.
  • bench_report.md regenerated each run (VC size pre/post DSA, median solver ms per feature bucket); diffed in PRs.
  • conformance.json is the authoritative ledger; the count of known_fail must trend down, never silently up — a new known_fail requires a justifying reason=.
  • Any new feature in later work MUST land with ≥1 PASS + ≥1 FAIL fixture; missing-fixture lint blocks merge. Soundness gate is non-negotiable and never waived.

Threat Model

Adversary verifies an annotated source program (source.spv) and trusts the spv verdict. The verifier is in the trusted computing base (TCB) of every downstream consumer: a false "Verified" is silently shipped. The external SMT solver is a second, semi-trusted oracle. This section enumerates trust boundaries, ranks threats by blast radius, and binds each to mitigation / detection / telemetry / recovery. Soundness is the cardinal property (see canonical facts): the VCGen must NEVER let a buggy program verify; everything else is availability or robustness.

26.1 Assets & trust boundaries

Asset Why it matters Owner
Verdict VerifyResult::Verified downstream treats as proof of {requires} body {ensures} spv-verify
VcFormula (valid iff correct) the proof obligation; soundness lives here spv-vc
IvlCmd desugaring (assert/havoc placement) dropping/mis-framing => unsound spv-ivl
SMT encoding VcFormula -> SmtTerm mis-encoded operator => unsound spv-smt
Counterexample bindings drives user trust in Failed verdict spv-verify 
UNTRUSTED                    | TRUSTED (TCB)                          | SEMI-TRUSTED
source.spv (annotated)  --B1-> spv-syntax/spv-ast/spv-ivl/spv-vc/spv-smt --B2-> SmtSolver (#278 / Z3)
solver answer (Unsat/Sat/Unknown) <----------------------- B2 -------------- (external process / lib)
deps (crates.io)        --B3-> compiled spv binary
  • B1 (parse boundary): every byte of source.spv is hostile. Front end is no_std+alloc, Result-only (ParseError/TypeError); no panic, no UB, no resource exhaustion on adversarial input.
  • B2 (solver boundary): the SmtSolver trait is the only channel to the solver. The verifier trusts Unsat to mean validity. A lying or buggy solver breaks soundness unless cross-checked.
  • B3 (supply chain): transitive crates run at build/verify time. deny.toml + minimal deps bound this.

26.2 Threat catalog (ranked by severity)

# Threat Boundary Vector Severity
T1 Unsoundness — buggy program reports Verified B1+TCB dropped/weakened Assert, under-Havoc, mis-encoded operator, lost old(), missing trigger-free axiom Critical
T2 Solver DoS / non-termination B2 huge quantified VC, matching loop, recursive-function axiom w/o fuel High (availability)
T3 Parser / typecheck panic or OOM B1 deeply nested Expr/Block, pathological tokens, giant literals High (availability)
T4 VC blowup → memory exhaustion TCB naive wp over Assign+Choice (exponential Q[e/x]) High (availability)
T5 Malicious / compromised SMT backend B2 returns Unsat for a Sat formula (or vice-versa) Critical (if T1-grade)
T6 Supply-chain compromise B3 malicious transitive crate, yanked/typo-squat High
T7 Counterexample exfiltration / confusion TCB mis-mapped DSA/skolem name → wrong Counterexample.bindings/at Medium (integrity of diagnostics)
T8 Unknown masquerading as success B2/TCB timeout/incompleteness silently treated as Verified Critical (soundness-adjacent)
T9 Vacuous specification / inconsistent precondition TCB requires false or mutually-inconsistent requires clauses; method body never reachable so trivially Verified High (soundness of claims)
T10 Regression after valid refactor B1+TCB renaming a variable in the spec without updating all modifies / ensures old() references; silently changes meaning without parse/type error Medium (correctness of annotations)

26.3 T1 — Unsoundness (the gravest threat) — soundness analysis

Unsoundness = the VC fails to capture some way the program violates its spec, so (NOT VC) is Unsat though a real counterexample exists. Failure loci, each a soundness obligation:

Locus Concrete bug Why unsound Guard (this blueprint)
Desugar (Phase 4–6) If→Choice omits one branch's assume; loop omits assert(inv) on entry; call omits assert(pre) or havoc(modifies) drops a proof obligation / over-constrains state per-rule unit fixtures; review checklist in Phase 13; mutation test (delete each Assert/Havoc → suite MUST flip to Failed)
wp (Phase 7) wp(assert p,Q)=p AND Q mis-coded as p=>Q (assume rule); wp(havoc x,Q)=forall x.Q drops binder; wp(c1[]c2) uses Or not And weakens VC wp algebraic-law tests; differential vs hand-computed VC on golden methods
passify/DSA (Phase 8) fresh-version renaming loses a write; merge at Choice join drops a version constraint a write becomes invisible DSA round-trip property: wp(c,Q) and wp(passify(c),Q) SMT-equivalent on fixtures
SMT encode (Phase 9) Int overflow modeled as wraparound, //mod semantics off-by-one, Map select/store axioms wrong, quantifier polarity flipped VC means something weaker than intended per-operator encode tests against SMT-LIB reference semantics; print-smt golden files
Frame (Phase 5,12) modifies/heap Map framing assumes too much unchanged callee can violate caller assumption frame-rule fixtures; ModifiesViolation expected-FAIL cases

Detection (primary defense): the Phase 13 soundness suite — a corpus of expected-FAIL (buggy) programs covering every VErrorKind; soundness invariant: zero expected-FAIL program may report Verified (a single such regression fails the "done" gate). Reinforced by (a) differential testing vs a Boogie/Dafny subset (any disagreement where spv says Verified but reference says fail = soundness bug), (b) mutation testing of the VCGen (each dropped Assert/Havoc/binder must be caught), (c) fuzzing of desugar→wp→emit for crashes and for "Verified on randomly-mutated buggy seed". Telemetry: CI emits verified_count vs expected_fail_count; any expected-FAIL→Verified logged as SOUNDNESS_REGRESSION (build-breaking). Recovery: revert offending VCGen commit; add the witness program as a permanent regression fixture. Completeness is explicitly secondary — a spurious Failed (false alarm) is annoying, not dangerous, and is tracked in a separate completeness ledger.

26.4 T2 / T8 — Solver DoS, non-termination, and Unknown

Aspect Decision
Vector quantified VC with bad/missing Trigger → matching loop; recursive pure function inlined unboundedly
Mitigation per-method timeout (default 10 s, configurable); fuel bounds recursive-function unfolding; trigger discipline — every Forall carries explicit Vec<Trigger> (Phase 3/9), no trigger-less quantifiers reach the solver; passify keeps VC linear-ish so the solver isn't handed exponential input
Unknown handling SmtResult::Unknown is NEVER mapped to Verified (Phase 10). Maps to a non-success outcome surfaced to the user (incomplete/timeout), distinct from Failed. This closes T8 — soundness does not depend on the solver succeeding, only on never trusting an unjustified Unsat.
Detection per-method solver wall-time + result histogram; alert when Unknown/timeout rate exceeds threshold
Telemetry log (method, smt_term_count, solver_ms, SmtResult) per discharge
Recovery incremental push/pop (Phase 10) isolates one method; a runaway method times out without stalling the batch; lower fuel/add Trigger and re-run

26.5 T3 / T4 — Front-end and VC resource exhaustion

  • T3 (parser/typeck robustness): spv-syntax/spv-ast are total functions to Result<_, ParseError|TypeError> — no panic!, unwrap, or unbounded recursion on user input. Recursive-descent parser bounds nesting depth (configurable cap; exceed → ParseErrorKind::UnexpectedToken-class error, not stack overflow). Detection: cargo fuzz targets on Lexer/Parser/TypeChecker (Phase 13) assert "no panic, no OOM, terminates". Recovery: malformed input rejected with a span; verifier process survives.
  • T4 (VC blowup): root cause is naive wp duplicating Q across Assign/Choice (exponential, Flanagan-Saxe). Mitigation: mandatory passify (DSA) before wp (Phase 8) — assignments become assume of equalities, Choice shares the continuation, VC size linear-ish in program size. Hard size caps: VcFormula node count and SmtTerm count ceilings (configurable); exceed → abort that method with a resource-limit diagnostic, never silent Verified. Telemetry: emit vc_node_count, smt_term_count per method; alert on caps. Target: VC node count within a small constant factor of IVL size for loop/branch-bounded methods.

26.6 T5 — Untrusted SMT backend

The solver sits behind the SmtSolver trait (fn check(&mut self, &[SmtTerm]) -> SmtResult). A compromised/buggy backend that returns Unsat for a satisfiable (NOT VC) yields a false Verified — a T1-grade outcome the verifier cannot detect from the bare answer.

Control Description
Trait boundary sole channel; the verifier emits SMT-LIB 2 (Phase 9) and consumes only Unsat/Sat(Model)/Unknown — no backend-specific trust
Optional proof / UNSAT-core checking for Unsat, request the solver's UNSAT core / proof and validate it covers the asserted SmtTerms; mismatch → treat as Unknown, not Verified
Model double-check for Sat, the reconstructed Model (Phase 11) is re-evaluated against the VC atoms; a model that does not actually falsify the VC flags a backend/encoding fault (logged, downgraded from Failed)
Backend diversity run two backends (#278 and Z3) on the soundness suite in CI; disagreement = investigate before trusting either

Detection: CI cross-backend differential; per-verdict optional core check. Recovery: pin a known-good solver version; quarantine a backend that fails core checks.

26.7 T6 — Supply chain

  • Minimal dependency surface: the seven spv-* crates target a tiny external footprint; spv-syntax..spv-smt are no_std+alloc where stated, limiting transitive pulls.
  • deny.toml (Phase 0 CI): bans, license allow-list, advisory DB (cargo-deny), duplicate-version checks; build fails on a flagged or yanked crate.
  • Pinned MSRV 1.78 + locked Cargo.lock; reproducible Docker build (Phase 0) for verify-time determinism.
  • Detection: CI cargo deny check + cargo audit on every push. Recovery: pin/replace the offending crate; rebuild the locked image.

26.8 T7 — Counterexample integrity

Counterexample.bindings: Vec<(Atom, Value)> and at: Span must map DSA/skolem names back to the original source variable and the actual failing program point (Phase 11), with the right VErrorKind. A mis-mapping is not unsound (the verdict is still Failed) but erodes trust and can hide the real bug. Mitigation: maintain a name-provenance map through passify; the Reason{kind, span} tag on each Assert (Phase 4) pins the source VErrorKind+Span. Classic usability pitfall (canonical): a weak loop invariant makes the inductive assert(inv) fail, producing a counterexample to the user's invariant, not a real program bug — the rendered VError must say LoopInvariantNotMaintained (not AssertionMightNotHold) so the user fixes the annotation. Detection: counterexample-fidelity fixtures assert (kind, at, bindings) exactly. Telemetry: log VErrorKind distribution.

26.9 Out-of-scope (per NON-GOALS)

Not defended here: side-channel/timing leakage of program contents; multi-tenant isolation of the spv process; concurrency/separation-logic adversaries; execution of verified programs (spv never runs the program). These are explicit non-goals; the threat surface is bytes-in (source.spv) and the solver answer only.

Failure Modes & Recovery

Taxonomy of how spv fails, ordered by severity. F-1 (unsoundness) is the gravest — every other class merely costs a user a result; F-1 silently certifies a buggy program. Severity tiers: S0 = soundness compromised (release-blocking, page on-call); S1 = usability/availability (correct program unverifiable or spv hangs/crashes); S2 = input rejected with diagnostic (working as designed, low severity).

ID Class Tier Stage Default outcome
F-1 Unsound verification (buggy program → Verified) S0 spv-vc / spv-smt / spv-ivl false Verified — silent
F-2 Passive-form (DSA) bug altering VC validity S0 spv-vc passify feeds F-1 or F-3
F-3 Spurious failure (correct program → Failed) S1 spv-ivl / spv-vc / spv-smt false VError + bogus Counterexample
F-4 Solver Unknown / timeout S1 spv-smt / spv-verify VError{MightNotTerminate?} or explicit Unknown
F-5 Matching loop / quantifier non-termination S1 spv-smt solver wall-clock hang → Unknown
F-6 Counterexample does not re-execute (model-decode bug) S1 spv-verify misleading Counterexample
F-7 Spec well-formedness error S2 spv-ast TypeError::{NonBoolSpec, OldOutsideEnsures, BadModifies}
F-8 Parse / type error S2 spv-syntax / spv-ast ParseError / TypeError
F-9 Driver / IO / OOM panic S1 spv-cli / spv-verify process abort, nonzero exit
F-10 Vacuous proof (inconsistent precondition accepted silently) S0 (soundness of claim) spv-verify / spv-ivl Verified reported; method body unreachable; vacuity probe required

F-1 — Unsound verification (S0, cardinal failure)

  • Symptom: VerifyResult::Verified for a program that violates its spec at runtime; no diagnostic — failure is silent by construction.
  • Root cause (enumerated): (a) dropped/skipped IvlCmd::Assert in desugar_method (postcondition, call-pre, or loop-inv assert never emitted); (b) over-broad Havoc(Vec<Var>) — havocing a var that isn't in the loop targets / modifies, erasing a fact that should constrain the VC; (c) under-broad havoc — failing to havoc a loop target or modifies member, so a stale value masks the bug; (d) wrong wp rule, e.g. wp(assert p,Q)=p⇒Q instead of p∧Q, or wp(assume p,Q)=p∧Q instead of p⇒Q; (e) mis-encoded operator in VcFormula→SmtTerm (signed vs Euclidean mod, Int overflow assumed, Map select/store axiom wrong); (f) passify (F-2) producing a non-equivalent VC; (g) trigger so restrictive a needed quantifier instantiation never fires on the negated VC path, letting Sat-that-should-be collapse to Unsat — rare but possible via inconsistent axioms.
  • Detection: the soundness benchmark suite (Phase 13) — every expected-FAIL program MUST report Failed; a single expected-FAIL that returns Verified is a release blocker. Plus differential vs Boogie/Dafny subset (disagreement where they say "error", we say "verified"). Plus a --audit invariant: assert the count of emitted IvlCmd::Assert ≥ (postconditions + call-sites×callee-pres + loops×invariants). Plus property test: for any well-typed program, wp(c, false) must be unsatisfiable-implying only via genuine assume false (vacuity probe, see Failure Analysis below).
  • Automatic recovery: none at runtime — unsoundness cannot self-detect. The only automatic guard is CI: the soundness suite is the done gate; merge is blocked on 100% expected-FAIL coverage. A canary set of historically-missed bugs (regression corpus) runs on every commit.
  • Manual recovery: bisect the pipeline with print-ivl → print-vc → print-smt; locate the missing Assert/Havoc or wrong rule; add a minimized reproducer to the soundness corpus before fixing (red-then-green); fix; re-run full differential.
  • Operator actions: treat any field report of "tool said verified but it crashed/violated spec" as S0; yank the affected spv version; freeze releases until the reproducer is in the suite and green.
  • Forensic artifacts: persist per-method IvlCmd (print-ivl), VcFormula (print-vc), emitted SMT-LIB (print-smt), the asserted-count audit log, solver version + seed. These let a reviewer replay the exact VC offline.
  • Long-term mitigation: keep VCGen small and exhaustively match-ed (no catch-all arms that could silently skip a Stmt/IvlCmd variant); independent VC re-checker that re-derives wp by a second implementation and diffs the VcFormula; proof-carrying option (emit Unsat core, validate it refutes the negated VC); grow the differential corpus continuously. Soundness > completeness, always.

F-2 — Passive-form (DSA) bug altering VC validity (S0)

  • Symptom: VC validity flips after passify — a method that should fail verifies (→ F-1) or one that should verify fails (→ F-3); manifests as print-vc before/after passify being logically inequivalent.
  • Root cause: version-numbering errors in DSA — reusing a version index, wrong join after Choice (the two branches must reconcile to a common max version with assume vₖ = vᵢ/vⱼ φ-style equalities), or turning Assign(x,e) into Assume(x_k = e) against the wrong version of x in e.
  • Detection: equivalence harness — for a corpus of IvlCmd, check wp(c, Q) and wp(passify(c), Q') are equisatisfiable (modulo renaming) via the solver itself; fuzz passify (Phase 13) on random Seq/Choice/Assign trees and assert SMT-equisat.
  • Automatic recovery: none; passify is purely a perf transform (Flanagan-Saxe) — on any equivalence-check failure in debug builds, fall back to non-passive wp (correct but exponential) and emit a WARN passify-disabled so results stay sound at the cost of speed/size.
  • Manual recovery: diff the pre/passify VcFormula; inspect version map; fix join logic; add the tree to the passify fuzz corpus.
  • Operator actions: if WARN passify-disabled appears in prod, expect VC-size/solver-time regressions; route to verifier team — it's an S0 latent bug even though current results are sound.
